# PROGRESS.md — PostMind Studio Build Log

One line per completed backlog item. Newest at the top.

**Format:** `[YYYY-MM-DD] [phase.item] Short description of what was done.`

---

## Phase 26

[2026-10-09] [26.2] Launch polish audit: every public page has a canonical URL, og:url, its own localised description (sign-in, sign-up, forgot-password, each legal document; 11 locales) and both preview images (src/lib/seo/page-metadata.ts; the home page's own openGraph had dropped og:image); twitter-image route; OG image set in Geist 400/600 (static TTFs built offline from the fontsource WOFF2 by scripts/fonts/geist-og-instances.mjs) with the new lockup; noindex on forgot/reset-password, verify-email, two-factor, invite and the signed-in app; robots.txt lists each private section once; sitemap adds sign-in/sign-up and lastmod. New brand artwork (assets/brand → scripts/brand/build-brand-assets.mjs → public/brand icon + light/dark lockups, src/app icon.png / apple-icon.png / favicon.ico) in the marketing header and footer, app sidebar (icon in the collapsed rail and phone header), auth frame, Meta deletion page, 404 (now under the site header), OG image and emails (hosted logo by APP_URL); manifest.ts. Marketing header keeps the lockup on phones (switchers move to the footer below sm). Code audit: approvals workflow titles h2 (no h1→h3 jump); Create's format tiles rounded-lg like the other tiles and "More options" / "Change model" on the Button primitive; skeletons instead of spinners for the automation wizard and the public preview page; "Show all" actions on filtered empty project and publication lists; px font sizes normalised to the rem scale; long unbroken user text wraps (body overflow-wrap). Billing/pricing paths untouched (pricing page metadata listed for the billing branch). Tests: seo, public-metadata, manifest, opengraph-image, not-found, brand-mark, marketing header, sidebar, emails, approvals, create, automations, share, projects/publications lists, design tokens.
[2026-10-09] [26.1] Tiered pricing (Starter/Growth/Pro) replaces the 21.5 per-channel plan (operator decision 2026-10-09, "apply new pricing"). New pure `billing/plans.ts` (replaces `channel-plan.ts`): Starter £29 / Growth £69 / Pro £149 a month excl. VAT; weekly = monthly ÷ 4 × 1.3 rounded UP to 50p (£9.50 / £22.50 / £48.50); yearly 10 × monthly (£290 / £690 / £1,490); HD videos 8 / 20 / 45 a calendar month (yearly released per calendar month) or 2 / 5 / 11 an ISO week (weekly); businesses 1 / 1 / 3; seats 1 / 3 / 10; Growth "Most popular"; plan names are product names (never translated); internal tier STANDARD on every plan; caps = the plan's most videos in a calendar month × 241p × 1.25 (weekly × 5 weeks), daily half; change timing: a higher plan now (prorated), a lower plan at period end, same plan longer interval now / shorter at period end. Every plan posts to every platform: the channel limit is gone (`billing/channels.ts`, `ChannelLimitError` / 403 `channel_limit`, the automation network limit and the Blitz over-limit targets removed). Stripe: one product per plan (`studio_plan_<plan>`), lookup keys `studio_<plan>_<weekly|monthly|yearly>`, quantity 1; packs 5 for £17, 15 for £45 (same keys, new amounts via `transfer_lookup_key`). Trial 7 days (`STUDIO_TRIAL_DAYS` default 7), 2 HD videos; trial caps unchanged (£10 a day, £15 total). Entitlements carry `plan {id, interval, source}` (seats and businesses from the plan; staff custom limits win); stored `derived.plan` (rows written before 26.1 with `channels` are read as the mapped plan); admin overrides set `plan` + interval. Back-compat: a subscription still on `studio_channel_*` maps by quantity (1 Starter, 2–3 Growth, 4+ Pro) everywhere (entitlements, Your plan, emails, admin) until `scripts/billing/migrate-to-tiers.ts` (new, dry run by default, `--apply`, idempotent, schedule released first, quantity 1, `proration_behavior=none`, same interval; also maps any old Basic / Standard / Plus price; replaces `migrate-channel-plans.ts`; logic in `billing/tier-migration.ts`, tested with a fake Stripe client). No database migration (plan derived from the lookup key). API: checkout `{kind:'plan', plan, interval}`; `POST /billing/plan {plan, interval, prorationDate?}` and `GET /billing/plan/preview?plan=&interval=` (zod, unknown plans / intervals / the old `channels` body → 400); overview `plan {id, interval, …, pending {plan, interval, effectiveAt}}` (no `channels`); `/me` `plan.studioPlan`; usage and quota errors flag `studioPlan`; quota messages say "upgrade your plan or buy a video pack"; trial-ending email names the plan. Admin: subscriptions rows and `byPlan` summary, organisations list / detail show the plan. UI: plan picker (Starter / Growth / Pro, Growth most popular, Weekly / Monthly / Yearly "2 months free", videos, quick posts, businesses, seats, "Posts to every platform") on Your plan, /pricing and the landing; "Your channels" section and Connections channel notice removed; 11 locales. Legal: terms 6.1 / 6.2 / 6.3 / 6.5 / 7.4 and the AUP updated to the plans (6.6 Channels removed). Demo on Growth monthly. Runbooks billing-stripe.md (§1, §1a 26.1 order, §2, §5 margins) and go-live.md 8.2. Nothing was run against Stripe.
- OPERATOR after deploy: (1) `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY=sk_test_… npx tsx scripts/billing/seed-stripe-test.ts --dry-run`, then without `--dry-run`; (2) `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY=sk_test_… DATABASE_URL=… npx tsx scripts/billing/migrate-to-tiers.ts`, then with `--apply`; (3) archive the `studio_channel_*` prices in the dashboard. Margins at the internal cap: Starter monthly −2.0 %, Growth +2.1 %, Pro +1.7 %, every yearly plan −16 to −21 % (typical use 39–66 % everywhere); runbook §5.

## Phase 25

[2026-10-08] [25 polish] Wall-of-text balanced line breaks: production renders left a ragged block ("opener," or "fire" alone on the last line) because Shotstack and the local renderer wrapped the block greedily. New src/lib/studio/overlays/balanced-wrap.ts breaks each owner line at the box's estimated characters per line (overlays/shotstack.ts charsPerLine, the same 0.56 em estimate as overlayBoxHeight) into the same number of lines greedy would use, with the least sum of squared line lengths (min-raggedness DP) and a heavy penalty on a one-word last line; words longer than a line are cut as greedy does; owner line breaks are kept; Han/Kana/Hangul text is left to the renderer. overlayClip takes balanceLines and compose-video.ts sets it for WALL_OF_TEXT projects, so the rich-text clip carries explicit line breaks and both Shotstack and the local renderer (which draws from the same edit) show even lines; other overlays are unchanged. Tests: balanced-wrap.test.ts (orphan avoidance, same line count as greedy, limits and word order, long words, empty/one-word, owner breaks, Han), shotstack.test.ts and compose.test.ts (balanceLines on/off).
[2026-10-08] [25 polish] Demo posters: render posters in the demo are the real Studio-made showcase posters (public/marketing/studio, already inlined for the landing page through demo/shims/marketing-media-src.ts, so the bundle does not grow) instead of a flat gradient. demo/media.ts samplePoster(seed, kind) picks one per project (food and high-street slideshows / Seedance clips for videos, the wall-of-text posters for WALL_OF_TEXT), draws it with the SAMPLE mark once decoded and preloads them at start-up; GET /renders/:id thumbnailUrl (review player, variant cards, analytics) uses it and a regenerated thumbnail puts the overlay text over the poster. New demo handler GET /projects/:id/preview (demo/api/handlers/p25-post-preview.ts) so the calendar side panel shows a made post's poster (or a post in progress's storyboard stills) instead of its error state. Tests: test/unit/demo-posters.test.ts.
[2026-10-07] [25.10] Library and My media (builds on #137). MediaTile primitive (src/components/ui/media-tile.tsx, deferred from 25.3): one link or button named by its visible title and described by its meta line, a frame with a declared aspect (9:16 / 16:9 / 1:1 / 4:5; cover, or contain on a quiet matte for mixed grids), lazy poster with width/height, Geist Mono duration badge, decorative play affordance for video, status / licence pill (top start), optional badge (bottom start), hover / focus preview only when the caller can supply one (after 350 ms, never under reduced motion). Reference library (/library): recommended shelf as a horizontal snap row, one calm toolbar (search first, category, length, mood, tags; category and length apply at once, text on Enter / Apply) kept in the URL (?q=&category=&length=&mood=&tags=, router.replace, deep links and reload work, cursor pages reset when filters change), responsive grid of 9:16 tiles (2 → 6 columns) with licence pill, match score and the existing muted hover preview from the detail API, styled Previous / Page n / More references. Detail (/library/[id]): the player large on a matte stage (aspect-correct, sound on demand, no download as before) beside the facts (mono numbers), tags and the existing "Use as reference" TEMPLATE / INSPIRE links; blueprint timeline with a time ruler; similar shelf. My media (/media, new (studio) page, PROTECTED_PAGE_PREFIXES, nav Library → My media, so the command menu lists it): one newest-first feed from the new read-only GET /api/studio/media (services/media.ts; requireTenantContext + studio:project:read, organisation-scoped and business-scoped with ?businessId=, ?type=all|video|image|upload, keyset cursor on (createdAt, id) across the three tables, limit ≤ 60): finished renders (quality PASSED / FORCE_APPROVED of live projects, thumbnail signed like GET /renders/:id), READY uploaded videos (source + demo videos, signed playback URL) and the image library (presented as GET /image-library does, only while the image-library feature is on). Segments All / Videos / Images / Uploads in ?type=, the business from the top bar (or every business, said so), 4:5 contain tiles with type / duration / status badges, preview dialog (render via GET /renders/:id/preview, upload via its signed URL, image whole; details; Download via GET /renders/:id/download with studio:render:download, or the signed URL; Open project; focus returns to the tile), Previous / More paging, empty state per segment pointing to where uploads already happen (Create, Business & images); no new uploader. Catalogue: 49 keys × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Demo: /media route and GET /media from the demo projects, uploads and image library. Tests: ui/media-tile.test.tsx, media/media-model.test.ts, media/media-screen.test.tsx, library-browse URL filter cases, services/media.test.ts, test/api/media.test.ts on PGlite (capability, newest-first merge, cross-organisation isolation, business + type filters, paging, bad cursor / limit / type, image library switched off); e2e library spec checks the URL filters survive a reload; sweep and responsive key pages visit /media.
[2026-10-07] [25.8 review + Image Studio] Player-first project review and Image Studio (builds on #136). Review (/projects/[id], review-screen.tsx split into review-player, review-status, attention-model/attention-list, pipeline-model, project-menu, review-tab-content, use-tab-param): from lg the chosen variant plays large on the left (aspect-correct frame, the 15.A3 thumbnail as poster, native controls, one chip per format; a placeholder while the first format renders) and a status & actions column sits beside it — one status line (state pill + the 24.2 live StatusChip with ETA while in progress via useLiveRefetch, polling only without the stream; a polite sr-only status announces the state and how many things need attention), the pipeline strip, the next action (Generate / Generate again / Cancel, Approve / Reject via the existing ApprovalBar, Publish / Posts and schedule → the Publish tab), the approval-step indicator, one "Needs your attention" list and quiet details (automation summary, music, sound effects). A draft or a stopped run with no video drops the player column. Phones: player, status, tabs. Actions menu: Download video (the variant in the player), Share for feedback (ShareLinksPanel in a dialog), Save as template (SaveTemplate in a dialog; removed from the automation panel there), Duplicate (opens the copy), Archive / Unarchive — same endpoints as before. Tabs (Variants/Slides/Carousel, Shots, Overlays, Script, Publish) live in ?tab= (router.replace, first tab keeps the URL clean, unknown tabs ignored, deep links work); variant cards lose their own player and gain Watch. Pipeline strip: done / current / waiting / failed / not reached; FAILED shows the step it stopped at (from the failure code, else from how far scripts, shots and renders got) and DRAFT says "Not started — Generate to begin.". Image Studio /images (new (studio) page, PROTECTED_PAGE_PREFIXES, nav Create → Images, so the command menu lists it too): prompt / style / shape exactly as POST /image-library/generate accepts (no model or count controls), results = GET /image-library?source=generated (newer/older pages), a placeholder tile while generating, Generate again (same request), preview dialog (image, prompt, style, size, download via the signed preview URL, Edit prompt, Generate again); plan-locked (PlanLockBadge + "See what's included" opening the upgrade dialog, earlier images still shown), feature flag off and read-only states; no slideshow deep link exists, so none is offered. Business → Images keeps its dialog and links to Image Studio. Catalogue: 88 keys × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Demo: /images route, generated images stored like the real app (prompt as alt text, "Style:" line). Tests: review-layout.test.tsx (draft / generating / ready / failed / published, attention list, URL tab sync, chips + Watch, menu duplicate/download), attention-model.test.ts, pipeline-strip.test.tsx, images/image-studio-model.test.ts, images/image-studio-screen.test.tsx (submit, results, preview, generate again, locked, flag off, viewer); e2e sweep and responsive key pages visit /images.
[2026-10-07] [25.6] Sign-in and onboarding (builds on #135). Visual/UX layer only: Better Auth calls, redirects, safeNextPath, rate limits, the onboarding API (GET/PATCH /onboarding, same steps and payloads) and every route are unchanged. Auth frame (src/components/auth/auth-frame.tsx, used by src/app/(auth)/layout.tsx and the demo): from lg two panes — the form column (wordmark with the vermilion record dot, form max 400px) and a quiet brand panel with a one-line value statement over the real review screen (public/marketing/screens/review-light|dark.webp via imgProps, theme-matched with dark:, width/height declared, lazy); below lg one column; the language switcher stays in the top corner. Screens restyled on the 25.3 primitives: H1 on the canvas (no card chrome), helper text, one primary action (Button loading while pending), quiet secondary links; field errors (invalid email, password too short/long/breached, wrong 2FA code) now show under their field with aria-invalid + aria-describedby and take focus, other errors stay in one alert on top; autocomplete/inputmode on every field; 2FA is one input (inputmode numeric, autocomplete one-time-code) with the backup-code switch as a quiet action; forgot-password sent state ("Check your inbox", what happens next, "Use a different email"); reset success "Password changed"; verify-email inbox state and a calm bad-link state where "Send a new link" is the primary action; invite: an unusable invitation gets its own state (ask for a new invitation, Go to Studio); sign-up closed calm with sign-in as the way on. Google button: neutral (white / dark surface) per Google's branding; no G mark exists in the repo, so it stays text-only. /connections callback notices and the Meta data-deletion status page use the same calm tonal pattern (restyle only). Error + 404 (PageErrorView / NotFoundView): typographic composition (mono label, H1, what happened and what to do); 404 offers Home + projects when the session cookie is present (cookie name only, as the landing page), else the home page + sign-in; the Studio error boundary offers Try again, Go to Home and "Tell us what happened" (the shell's feedback dialog); root error goes to "/" (which forwards signed-in visitors to /home). Onboarding /welcome: "Step n of m" plus a segmented progress row (Organisation, Business, Brand, Channels, First video, Done; names hidden on phones but read out), each step on one quiet panel, Back / Skip for now / Continue in a footer that sticks to the bottom on phones (safe-area aware), focus moves to the new step panel after each move; brand-kit step has a live preview card (first palette colour with black/white text by contrast, chosen font, tone words) and a quiet link to the website scan on /business (the wizard has no scan step and the onboarding API has no step for it, so no step was invented); Channels lists all six platforms with a StatusPill (Connected / Needs reconnecting / Not connected) and account names; first video uses the shared Textarea and Button loading; Done is a composed success state (status mark, no confetti), "What happens next" (review, publish, Insights) with Go to Home and the month planner (/plans/new), then the posting plan card; finished state offers Home and another video. Catalogue: 37 new keys and 8 changed values × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Tests: auth-frame.test.tsx, brand-preview.test.tsx, new 25.6 cases in auth-screens.test.tsx (field error linked + focused, autocomplete, 2FA attributes, sent state, bad verify link, unusable invite, sign-up closed) and page-error.test.tsx (signed-in/out homes, feedback); wizard and i18n tests follow the new labels; e2e specs use "Skip for now".

No new upgrade prompts; the upgrade dialog host is unchanged. Google button: neutral (white / dark surface) per Google's branding; no G mark exists in the repo, so it stays text-only. /connections callback notices and the Meta data-deletion status page use the same calm tonal pattern (restyle only). Error + 404 (PageErrorView / NotFoundView): typographic composition (mono label, H1, what happened and what to do); 404 offers Home + projects when the session cookie is present (cookie name only, as the landing page), else the home page + sign-in; the Studio error boundary offers Try again, Go to Home and "Tell us what happened" (the shell's feedback dialog); root error goes to "/" (which forwards signed-in visitors to /home). Onboarding /welcome: "Step n of m" plus a segmented progress row (Organisation, Business, Brand, Channels, First video, Done; names hidden on phones but read out), each step on one quiet panel, Back / Skip for now / Continue in a footer that sticks to the bottom on phones (safe-area aware), focus moves to the new step panel after each move; brand-kit step has a live preview card (first palette colour with black/white text by contrast, chosen font, tone words) and a quiet link to the website scan on /business (the wizard has no scan step and the onboarding API has no step for it, so no step was invented); Channels lists all six platforms with a StatusPill (Connected / Needs reconnecting / Not connected) and account names; first video uses the shared Textarea and Button loading; Done is a composed success state (status mark, no confetti), "What happens next" (review, publish, Insights) with Go to Home and the month planner (/plans/new), then the posting plan card; finished state offers Home and another video. Catalogue: 37 new keys and 8 changed values × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Tests: auth-frame.test.tsx, brand-preview.test.tsx, new 25.6 cases in auth-screens.test.tsx (field error linked + focused, autocomplete, 2FA attributes, sent state, bad verify link, unusable invite, sign-up closed) and page-error.test.tsx (signed-in/out homes, feedback); wizard and i18n tests follow the new labels; e2e specs use "Skip for now".

[2026-10-07] [25.13] Admin centre (builds on #137). Visual/UX layer only: requirePlatformStaff, the StaffOnly / needs-2FA states, the kill-switch access probe and every admin API are unchanged. DataTable gains `rowHeader` (renders th scope=row via new TableRowHeader), restored on the cost tier-caps, queues and providers tables (lost in 25.3) and used for organisations, users and Stripe records; dense DataTables replace the raw tables in Organisations, Users and Stripe records; sorting where data is client-side (queues, providers incl. breaker order, users, records); numbers in Geist Mono; users' verified/2FA as StatusPills. Kill switch card: destructive start edge while running, destructive-soft wash when halted. Catalogue: 16 keys × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Tests: admin-centre.test.tsx (menu groups, link click writes ?tab with replace, phone picker, ?tab followed after load, legacy ?tab=subscriptions, Halted pill), primitives-25-3 rowHeader test, billing remove-override confirmation, i18n and p18 tests follow; e2e pass2-billing-drip-admin (every section loads, reload keeps the section), pass2-a11y, sweep and library-templates use the admin menu.

A draft or a stopped run with no video drops the player column. Phones: player, status, tabs. Needs your attention: every notice that stacked above the tabs is one compact row, exactly when its panel would have shown (same predicates): direction to choose, UGC rewrite, restricted topics, the failure (role=alert), project budget pause, org limit pause with auto-resume, safety review, automatic review / schedule issue, music or sound effects failed (warnings), queued for a busy provider, backup provider, presenter / actor unavailable (notes); action rows start open (anchors from the projects list still land), others expand to the existing panel. Pipeline strip: done / current / waiting / failed / not reached; FAILED shows the step it stopped at (from the failure code, else from how far scripts, shots and renders got) and DRAFT says "Not started — Generate to begin.". Image Studio /images (new (studio) page, PROTECTED_PAGE_PREFIXES, nav Create → Images, so the command menu lists it too): prompt / style / shape exactly as POST /image-library/generate accepts (no model or count controls), results = GET /image-library?source=generated (newer/older pages), a placeholder tile while generating, Generate again (same request), preview dialog (image, prompt, style, size, download via the signed preview URL, Edit prompt, Generate again); plan-locked (PlanLockBadge + "See what's included" opening the upgrade dialog, earlier images still shown), feature flag off and read-only states; no slideshow deep link exists, so none is offered. Business → Images keeps its dialog and links to Image Studio. Catalogue: 88 keys × 11 locales (translated, queued for review). Demo: /images route, generated images stored like the real app (prompt as alt text, "Style:" line). Tests: review-layout.test.tsx (draft / generating / ready / failed / published, attention list, URL tab sync, chips + Watch, menu duplicate/download), attention-model.test.ts, pipeline-strip.test.tsx, images/image-studio-model.test.ts, images/image-studio-screen.test.tsx (submit, results, preview, generate again, locked, flag off, viewer); e2e sweep and responsive key pages visit /images.

[2026-10-07] [25.9] Calendar, month planner, Blitz, automations and publications (builds on #137). **Calendar** (/calendar): Month / Week / Day views (SegmentedControl) over the same GET /publications from/to window, with view, day and filters in the URL (`?view=&date=&platform=&status=&source=`, calendar-view.ts + use-calendar-url.ts, replace not push); the desktop month is an ARIA grid (row per week, columnheaders, gridcells with roving focus: arrows / Home / End / PageUp / PageDown move and select the day, Enter or the date opens the Day view; `data-day` cells and drag-and-drop unchanged); up to four posts a day then "+n more" (Radix popover with everything on the day); week = seven columns, day = one wide column, phones = agenda (week/day list empty days quietly); post chips show time, a short network mark, the 24.2 StatusChip and the campaign ("Month plan · 1 Oct" / "Automation · name"); week/day chips load the thumbnail lazily (live event thumbnailUrl, else the existing GET /projects/:id/preview poster or first slide, only when one exists); client-side filters platform / status (incl. "Being made" month-plan posts) / source (month plan / automation / made by hand) with a "Showing n of m" status line; open slots only show unfiltered. Undo after every move (sonner action, 10 s; the same PATCH back to the old time) plus an sr-only live region. The drip queue moved off the bottom into a "Posting times" side sheet (inline end) opened from the header and from the "set posting times" link. **API (additive, no new routes):** GET /publications rows carry `campaign` (services/publication-campaigns.ts: two indexed queries per page, organisation-scoped); …/drip-queue/upcoming `planned[].automationId`; GET /automations rows carry `nextPostAt` (earliest QUEUED/GENERATING/READY/SCHEDULED item of an ACTIVE/GENERATING automation's current plan, one `distinct` query). **Month planner:** /plans/new is one prompt first (start date + length, default 30, max 31; the defaults read as a sentence; posts a day, mix, UGC, platforms and accounts behind "More options", opened by itself when one needs an answer) with a "What Studio will make" preview (count, video/slideshow split, dates, networks, a per-day dot strip, review-first and "made about 3 days before it goes out"). /plans/[id]: a week-by-week timeline (PlanTimeline, groupByWeek from the plan's first day); draft posts are selectable for bulk "New topics" and "Delete" and running plans get per-item Approve (POST /projects/:id/approve) and "Approve all waiting" — there are no bulk routes, so bulk-run.ts calls the per-item routes one at a time with a progress bar, a summary toast and failed items left selected; the rolling-generation line sits with the review window (CreatesAtText per item unchanged). plan-editor split into item-form, plan-draft-item, plan-item-row, plan-bulk-bar. **Blitz:** the deck fills its column (the card was shrinking to its content), calmer buttons and shadows, key-cap hints (← Skip · ↑ Edit first · → Keep, mirrored in RTL, hidden on phones), and a read-only role or account now says why keep/skip are off (audit finding) and the keep dialog says when the role cannot post. **Automations:** list rows with status pill, what it makes and Next post; wizard "Step n of 5" with a progress line and focus moved to each step's heading; detail shows a timeline of runs (latest open, older folded; posted n of m). **Publications:** state and platform filters in the URL (radio group, no tablist). i18n: all new strings in 11 locales (review lists regenerated). Tests: calendar-view, views (week/day/URL/filters/grid keys/+n more), undo, campaigns service, automations nextPostAt, plan bulk/timeline/preview, Blitz read-only, automations 25.9, publications URL; e2e specs updated (week view + Undo in calendar-analytics, Posting times sheet in pass2 drip and sweep, sweep publications radios, platform in the URL).
- NOT VERIFIED: the Playwright e2e specs (need the app + database); demo checked in light/dark at 390/1440.

[2026-10-07] [25.11] Analytics: summary first, scoped to the selected business (builds on #137). /analytics is a hierarchy instead of a card grid: a one-sentence summary (posts, views, engagement rate, the platform that led) and four headline numbers (views, watch time, engagement rate, shares and saves) from the overview; growth and reach (views / watch time per day) and engagement (interactions per day + likes/comments/shares/saves mix), each with the change against the previous period computed from the same timeseries fetched for 2 x days (no change shown when there was no previous activity; the headline numbers carry no delta because the API has no previous-period totals); top posts with render thumbnails (GET /renders/:id) opening the publication drill-down; platform comparison; best posting times (GET /analytics/best-times, was only in the publish panel) as a ranked list with its basis; staff-only spend unchanged (organisation-wide, now says so when a business is selected). Period in the URL (?days=7|90, 30 default, use-range-param.ts). Tests: shell/nav.test.ts (every nav href is a real page, every (studio) section reachable, breadcrumbs), command-model.test.ts, command-menu.test.tsx (shortcut, focus return, filtering, arrows + Enter, debounced project search via the stubbed list API, business/theme actions, nothing fake: every listed href is a page), notice-strip.test.tsx, sidebar.test.tsx (groups, Create vs Get started, rail persisted, storage blocked), home-screen.test.tsx, updated app-shell.i18n / account-shell / page-guard tests; e2e specs use e2e/qa/shell.support.ts (account menu by data-shell attributes) and the sweep visits /home.

[2026-10-07] [25.x.1] Library matches must fit the slide too. After #132 the production re-run still showed the gym's puppies, the boutique's running child and the florist's vintage truck: the pre-fix run had stored those stock photos in each business's library, and `libraryMatch` (pgvector, searched before stock) reused them because only stock hits were checked. New `slideshow/library-relevance.ts`: `libraryMatch` takes the run's relevance gate and considers up to 3 matches ≥ MIN_SIMILARITY in similarity order. The business's own pictures (`ImageSource` UPLOAD, SCRAPED) are trusted with no call. Auto-stored STOCK and GENERATED pictures go through one batched yes/no check (stored objects read via `storage.size/readRange`, hotlinked Unsplash rows downloaded with safeGet, then 384 px thumbnails). The first own or passing match is used; none → stock → generation → text card. Rejected pictures are not deleted or marked (nothing recorded across organisations). `image-relevance.ts` is generalised (`checkRelevance` over image loaders, `createRelevanceGate`, `stockScreenOf`); library and stock checks share one per-slideshow cap, raised from 8 to 12 calls (≈ ≤ $0.018); an outage or the cap accepts the best match as before. Carousel callers of `libraryMatch` are unchanged (no gate). Tests: library-relevance.test.ts (own trusted, rejected stock → undefined, order, outage, candidate cap + business scope, storage vs hotlink loaders); populate-relevance.test.ts (library STOCK rejected → fitting stock photo, own upload without a check, outage accepts the match, shared cap).

[2026-10-07] [25.x] Music never silent under provider limits (production 2026-10-07: six slideshows generated at the same moment; ElevenLabs Music answered one with `rate_limited: too_many_concurrent_requests … maximum of 2 concurrent requests`, `produceMusic` recorded `music = { status: 'failed' }` and the slideshow rendered with no audio, −70 LUFS, QUALITY_FAILED, while a video of the same batch reused a 23.1 library track). Root cause: (1) 23.2 pooled ElevenLabs Music into the ElevenLabs account cap of 5 (org share 3), but the Music endpoint allows only 2 concurrent requests, so three music calls could be in flight; (2) `pipeline/music.ts` swallowed every music error into `failed` (no fallback, and a Studio-side `RateDeferredError` would have been swallowed too). Fix: **concurrency** — `providers/provider-concurrency.ts` `concurrencyScopesOf()`: a pooled provider holds a slot of its own cap AND of its pool under one lease id (taken narrowest first, all-or-nothing: a full pool gives the music slot back; `releaseLease` frees both), in the Redis and the in-process limiter; `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_CONCURRENCY['elevenlabs-music'] = 2` (org share 1), overridable with `STUDIO_PROVIDER_CONCURRENCY_ELEVENLABS_MUSIC` (documented in .env.example); `produceMusic` rethrows `RateDeferredError`, so a full music slot delays the compose job (worker-host moveToDelayed, no attempt spent) and the track is generated when a slot frees. **Fallback** — new `pipeline/music-fallback.ts` (`musicFailureHandling`: rethrow RateDeferredError / CostCapPausedError / KillSwitchTriggeredError, fall back for everything else) and `music-library.ts` `pickFallbackTrack` (the video's prompt key at least as long as the video, then any key long enough, then the longest track — the edit loops a shorter one; closest bucket then least recently used; rotation size ignored; marked used); `music-library-asset.ts` shares the project-asset creation (object check, missing objects dropped) with the 23.1 rotation; up to 12 picks (CI fix: with 3, the first pipeline-e2e run went silent — earlier scenarios, each in its own process with its own temporary storage, had left library rows of the same prompt key whose objects were gone, and three dead picks exhausted the fallback before the live track; the scenario now starts from a one-track library and test/db/music.test.ts covers more dead rows than the old limit through the worker entry point). Recorded as `music.status 'reused'`, `providerId 'music-library'`, `costPence 0`, `fallbackFrom: <reason>` (the review screen already shows reused tracks); the asset is filed under the prompt key only for a same-key match, so a later re-render of a relaxed pick tries to generate again. DECISION: `STUDIO_MUSIC_LIBRARY=off` also turns the fallback off (the library is then never used); with no usable track the video renders without music as before, warned (no invented audio). **Retry safety** — a retried compose job of the same run reuses the asset that run recorded (`sameRunTrack`), so it never picks or pays twice; the refused request's provider job carries no cost and music cost is only recorded for generated tracks. Tests: provider-concurrency.test.ts (music cap 2 inside the pool of 5, the third music call waits while speech still has slots, org share 1, a full pool releases the music slot, releaseLease frees both, env override / off, Redis: one lease in both scopes and the release on refusal; the 23.2 pool test adjusted), music-fallback.test.ts (decision per error; pick order, relax, loop, exclusions; asset at cost 0 with fallbackFrom; fingerprint only for same-key; missing objects dropped; null and never throws), test/db/music.test.ts on PGlite (the production refusal → library track under the edit, status reused with fallbackFrom rate_limited, cost 0, retry of the run keeps one asset; empty library → narration only, failed). Stubbed pipeline scenario `music-rate-limit` (scripts/qa/pipeline-e2e.ts, added to the CI loop): every ElevenLabs Music submit is refused with rate_limited, the library holds one track of another key, the slideshow must reach review with the fallback at 0p and pass the quality gate (real FFmpeg loudness) — not run locally (no Redis 7 / FFmpeg here), CI runs it.
[2026-10-07] [25.x] Slideshow photos match the business. Production renders (2026-10-07) showed photos unrelated to the business and often to the caption, because `fillSlideImages` searched the library and stock with the bare caption. New `slideshow/visual-query.ts`: before any search, every photo slide whose imageQuery is missing or just repeats its caption gets a 3–6 word stock-photo phrase from the topic, the business profile (sub-niche, industry, products/services, image themes; marked as data) and the caption, in ONE light call per slideshow (text task `slide_image_query`, Haiku 4.5, strict JSON schema, ≤ 400 output tokens, ≈ $0.003). A different imageQuery (Blitz visual hints, user edits) is kept. Call failure → topic + caption + two image themes, de-duplicated, ≤ 100 chars (never the bare caption). The phrase drives the pgvector library match, the stock search and the generated-image prompt. New `slideshow/image-relevance.ts`: `stockImageForSlide` takes an optional screen that sees each source's hits before any is stored; the slideshow screen downloads the ≤ 3 candidates (SSRF-guarded safeGet), shrinks them to 384 px JPEG with sharp and asks the light model one yes/no per photo (text task `slide_image_check`, ≤ 120 output tokens, ≈ $0.0015 per slide, at most 8 checks per slideshow ≈ ≤ $0.012); rejected hits are never stored, all rejected → next source → generated image (MAX_GENERATIONS_PER_RUN / per-org daily / monthly budget unchanged) → text card; any thumbnail/routing/model/parse failure or the cap → the best candidate is accepted unchecked. Total new cost ≈ $0.005–0.015 (≈ 0.4–1.2p) per slideshow. Month plans (content-plan-run.ts) and Blitz (blitz/project-body.ts) no longer copy the caption/title into imageQuery (Blitz keeps its writer's visual hints); writeText no longer copies written text into it. Tests: visual-query.test.ts, image-relevance.test.ts, populate-relevance.test.ts (whole chain with stubbed providers: rejection → next candidate, none fits → generated image, budget spent → text card, outage → best candidate, check cap), populate.test.ts / slide-images.test.ts / project-body.test.ts / text-task tests updated.

## Phase 24

[2026-10-07] [24.2] Calendar-first editing, part 1 (operator-approved plan "faster and better than Fastlane", 2026-10-06). (1) **Live status over SSE** — `GET /api/studio/live/projects` (withStudioRoute: session → tenant → `studio:project:read` → rate limit; a new `RouteResult.raw` passes the stream through) streams `project` events {state, stage, progressPct, etaSec, startedAt, thumbnailUrl}. Writers announce `{ projectId }` only on the Redis pub/sub channel `<queuePrefix>:live:org:<organisationId>` from the one place pipeline state moves (`transitionProject`, which every stage move and `failProject` go through) and the publish roll-up (`live/announce.ts`, best effort, never awaited; installed by scripts/worker.ts and api/context.ts; tests and one-off scripts have no bus). Scoping: the route subscribes to the session's organisation channel only and re-reads every announced project with `organisationId` in the WHERE (`live/snapshot.ts`), so even a foreign id published on the member's own channel is dropped (route test `test/api/live-projects.test.ts`: two organisations connected at once, forged id). One shared subscriber connection per web process (reference-counted channels), 25 s `: ping` heartbeat, `retry: 5000`, stream ends itself after 10 min, unsubscribe on abort/cancel, bursts per project coalesced, 503 `live_unavailable` without a bus. `GET /live/projects/status?ids=` (≤ 100) gives starting statuses. (2) **Chips + ETA** — "Planning / Making clips / Composing / Ready / Scheduled / Posted / Failed" on calendar posts, month-plan posts and plan items; ETA "~N min" counts down in the browser from `metadata.generationStart.at` and the per-format p50s measured on production 2026-10-06 in one module (`live/eta.ts`: carousel 26 s, wall of text 81 s, slideshow 112 s, hook + demo 135 s, UGC 230 s, AI video 431 s), progress kept inside each stage's band. Calendar, `/plans/:id` and Blitz no longer poll while the stream is open (one debounced refresh per burst of events); without EventSource, after 3 errors or a 503 they fall back to their old intervals (calendar 60 s) and retry the stream after 2 min. (3) **Side panel** — a calendar post or month-plan post opens a Radix dialog at the inline end (full-height sheet on phones; logical CSS only; Esc, focus trap, focus returns to the card) with the preview, caption + hashtags, networks, scheduled time, live status and existing actions only: Approve (`POST /projects/:id/approve`), Reschedule (the calendar's move dialog → `PATCH /publications/:id`), Regenerate (`POST /projects/:id/generate`), Open project; a planned post without a project shows its topic, time, status and "Open the plan". `GET /api/studio/projects/:id/preview` (read-only) assembles it. (4) **Instant preview** — Remotion Player (https://www.remotion.dev/docs/player/player, read 2026-10-06; `remotion` + `@remotion/player` 4.0.533, exact; `acknowledgeRemotionLicense`, free licence for teams ≤ 3, operator approved) behind a `next/dynamic` client-only import; composition at the render's frame size per aspect (9:16 / 1:1 / 4:5 / 16:9): the finished render (`OffthreadVideo`), carousel PNG slides, slideshow slides with their own durations and the TikTok-classic text over a slow zoom, the wall-of-text block for its reading time, or the storyboard of a video still being made (shot stills where an IMAGE asset exists, else the shot's words). Strings in 11 locales (machine-written ones listed in each review list). `drip-queue/upcoming` planned posts now carry `projectId`.
- Bundle (`next build`, this branch): Remotion is only in lazily loaded chunks — core 232 kB raw / 69 kB gzip plus the Player and composition 87 kB raw / 26 kB gzip, fetched the first time a panel opens; none is in any route's First Load JS (/calendar 217 kB, /plans/[id] 214 kB, /blitz 207 kB; shared 102 kB).
- DECISION: the bus carries ids only and every event is re-read per subscriber, so the channel never holds customer content and isolation does not depend on the publisher.
- DECISION: project writes outside `transitionProject` (generate → QUEUED, approve, archive) are not announced; the acting browser refreshes itself and other viewers see the next pipeline transition (seconds later). Part 2 can announce them if needed.
- NOT VERIFIED on production or with a real Redis here (no Docker): the bus is tested with an in-memory pub/sub stand-in; the CI database job's Redis does not exercise it.
[2026-10-07] [24.1] fal review fixes (PR #127 review): the result is always read from the built `queue.fal.run/<endpoint>/requests/<id>/response` path (a status `response_url` is never followed, so the key only goes to known paths); the clip URL Layer 3 downloads must be https on fal's CDN (`fal.media`, `*.fal.media`, `*.fal.run`; https://fal.ai/docs/documentation/model-apis/fal-cdn.md, read 2026-10-07) without credentials, else a non-retryable failed poll whose message omits the query (providers/fal-output.ts); a missing request (404) and a submit without request_id are no longer retried (either could pay twice; the latter is logged with model + endpoint for reconciliation); cancel ignores only 404 and a 400 whose body says ALREADY_COMPLETED. Env helpers moved to fal-config.ts (the setup checker already reports a bad STUDIO_FAL_VIDEO_MODELS). Tests: fal-security.test.ts (forged response_url, off-CDN / http / credentialed / look-alike hosts, FAL_KEY never in errors or health reason, bare 429/5xx classes), fal.test.ts (cancel 400/401/503, 404 not retried, request_id log).

[2026-10-06] [24.1] fal.ai video provider, OPT-IN (plans/24.1-fal-video.md). providers/fal.ts (`FalAdapter`, providerId `fal`, already a kill-switch id) over fal's queue API (https://fal.ai/docs/model-apis/model-endpoints/queue, read 2026-10-06): POST https://queue.fal.run/<endpoint> with `Authorization: Key <FAL_KEY>`, job id `<model>:<t2v|i2v>:<request_id>` (validated; status/response/cancel URLs are built only from known endpoint ids, a status `response_url` is used only on queue.fal.run), GET …/status (IN_QUEUE / IN_PROGRESS running; COMPLETED + error/error_type failed; 404 result_expired), GET response → `video.url` (fal CDN, copied to the assets bucket at once by generate-asset as every clip); cancel only while IN_QUEUE (running = NotImplementedError, cost reservation kept); health check = the authenticated, unbilled platform pricing read (https://api.fal.ai/v1/models/pricing). providers/fal-models.ts: MiniMax H3 Max (`minimax/h3-max/text-to-video` / `image-to-video`, 0.92–15 s, 768P for 720p), LTX-2.3 Fast (`fal-ai/ltx-2.3/text-to-video/fast` / `image-to-video/fast`, 6–20 s even, 1080p, silent) and Veo 3.1 Lite (`fal-ai/veo3.1/lite` / `lite/image-to-video`, 4/6/8 s, 720p silent), schemas from each endpoint's OpenAPI; shots round UP to an offered length, 4:5 → 3:4 (H3) or 9:16, 1:1 only on H3 (else the model is skipped). pricing.ts FAL_VIDEO_USD_PER_SECOND with sources (H3 Max regular rate $0.08/s at 768p — a 40%-off promotion until 15 October is ignored; LTX $0.06/s, the higher of the two figures fal publishes; Veo Lite $0.03/s at 720p silent). Errors: 401/403 auth, 402 or "exhausted/insufficient balance" (fal documents no wording) insufficient_credits, 429 rate_limited, 422 invalid_request or content_policy (`content_policy_violation`), 504 timeout, runner_* / internal_error provider_unavailable. OPT-IN: default-registry registers fal only when `STUDIO_FAL_VIDEO_MODELS` lists models (comma list, preference order; unknown or repeated key or no FAL_KEY = ConfigurationError), platform key only (not BYOC); the router's existing BASIC `fal` slot (after Veo) and a new last slot on paid tiers (after Luma) stay `not_configured` otherwise, so default routing is unchanged. `createFalModelAdapter(model, { usdToGbpRate, env?, fetchImpl? })` targets one model for scripts / bake-offs. Setup checker validates FAL_KEY / STUDIO_FAL_VIDEO_MODELS; both env examples document them. Error mapping lives in providers/fal-errors.ts; `npm run gate2:fal -- <model>` (scripts/test-fal.ts) makes one paid clip with one model. Tests: fal.test.ts (submit bodies and costs per model, Key auth, fall-through order, error mapping table, poll / result / response_url guard / failures, cancel, health), fal-setup.test.ts (job ids, error types, env parsing, createFalModelAdapter, router with fal on BASIC and paid tiers), fal-models.test.ts, default-registry.test.ts, env-file.test.ts, router.test.ts. Not verified live (no key): fal's real status/response URLs for these endpoints and its out-of-balance wording; no fal simulation in the load-test harness.

## Phase 23

[2026-10-06] [23.5] Local ffmpeg renderer for slideshows and walls of text (operator 2026-10-06 "yes build both"; production: an 84-post month autopilot stalled on Shotstack `insufficient_credits`, and Shotstack dominated cheap-format time, render p50 49 s, wall of text 81 s / slideshow 112 s end to end, ~8p + credits a render). (1) **Renderer seam** (queue/workers/compose-video.ts → render/local/compose-local.ts): after every variant's edit is built, a SLIDESHOW or WALL_OF_TEXT variant is rendered locally when `deps.localRenderer` is set (create-deps.ts, `STUDIO_LOCAL_RENDER=on|off`, default on; validated in setup/env-file.ts, documented in .env.example and deploy/vps/.env.example) and ffmpeg runs (`ffmpeg -version`, once per process); the MP4 goes to the renders bucket under providerOutputKey(providerId `local-ffmpeg`), is probed and passed through masterStoredRender like a composer render, and is recorded as a video_renders row (costPence 0, composerJobId `local-ffmpeg:<provider job>`, composition.renderer {provider, renderMs}) plus a provider_jobs row (provider local-ffmpeg, operation composition, SUCCEEDED, durationMs, 0p); quality gate, review, multi-format and publishing are unchanged. An edit the renderer does not draw (NotImplementedError: a shape asset, a video's own audio, overlapping audio such as narration over music, a non-mp4 output, an unknown clip field) goes to Shotstack silently; any other local failure is logged, recorded as a FAILED local-ffmpeg provider job and the variant falls back to Shotstack in the same job (if Shotstack is unavailable too, the existing composition_failed reason applies). (2) **Same inputs**: render/local/timeline.ts reads the exact Shotstack edit the composer built (slideshow/edl.ts, pipeline/edl.ts, overlays/shotstack.ts, ai-label.ts) — html/rich-text/image/video/audio assets, start/length/fit/effect/transition/position/offset/opacity/scale/transform, timeline background, output resolution/aspect/fps/quality/scaleTo. (3) **Drawing** (render/local/prepare.ts, text-image.ts, ffmpeg-args.ts): pictures resized with sharp for Shotstack `fit` (crop = cover + centre crop, contain on the backdrop); Ken Burns via zoompan (zoomIn/zoomOut 1→1.15, slides pan a 1.15 zoom; Fast/Slow 0.25/0.08) on a 1.5× supersampled still; the bottom track as frame-exact normalised segments (fps, scale, setsar, format, tpad+trim, settb) joined with xfade (fade/wipeleft/slideleft/zoomin…, 0.5 s, Fast 0.3, Slow 0.8 — Studio's own overlay speed mapping) or concat for cuts, backdrop-colour gaps and fades from/to nothing; text cards (html `<p>`/`<small>` + CSS subset, rich-text font/stroke/shadow/background/align/line height) drawn as PNGs with the carousel pipeline (carousel/render.ts TextSetter exported, bundled fonts, font-metrics wrapping, the TikTok-classic outline as the stroke-colour line at 16 offsets), overlaid with timeline `enable`, fade-alpha in/out and slide/wave motion expressions; the wall-of-text background clip letterbox-cropped (22.6 `crop`), cover-scaled and looped with -stream_loop; music clips with volume/trim/afade (1 s) concatenated with anullsrc silence, two-pass loudnorm (mastering.ts constants, −14 LUFS, linear) when there is sound; libx264 veryfast crf 20 (medium quality 23), yuv420p, AAC 192k 48 kHz, +faststart, exact length; each aspect ratio is its own ffmpeg process under STUDIO_FFMPEG_MAX_CONCURRENT. ffmpeg-filters documentation cited in ffmpeg-args.ts (read 2026-10-06). Parity notes (Shotstack documents neither): crossfades between slides instead of Shotstack's dip through the backdrop; transition / Ken Burns / audio-fade lengths as above; text in families not bundled (the slideshow's default Arial) is drawn in Inter; overlay slide transitions are a short slide with a fade, scale-in / pop-in / typewriter / blur-in are drawn as fades; letter spacing and italic are not applied; each approximation is logged and stored with the provider job. Measured render time: not measurable here (no ffmpeg on the build machine) — CI's integration test logs it. Tests: render/local/*.test.ts (timeline reading of real slideshow and wall-of-text edits incl. refusals, html/rich-text cards, golden ffmpeg argv: inputs, segment chains, xfade offsets, zoompan per move, overlay windows/fades/motion, audio/loudnorm, 4 aspect ratios + 720p drafts; PNG text rendering with real sharp/Pango incl. outline and determinism; preparation with real sharp; renderer with a scripted ffmpeg; compose seam: success 0p, failure → fallback + FAILED job, unsupported, stale, skip reasons), test/integration/local-render-ffmpeg.test.ts (real ffmpeg on tiny generated inputs: 9:16 slideshow and 16:9 wall of text probed for size, duration, h264/aac, no blackdetect intervals, loudness in the gate window; skipped without ffmpeg), test/db/local-render-pipeline.test.ts (slideshow ×2 formats and wall of text reach READY_FOR_REVIEW with provider local-ffmpeg and 0p and no Shotstack request; a failing local render falls back to Shotstack and still reaches review; renderer off → Shotstack as before).

[2026-10-06] [23.4] Month plans in batches + self-healing automations + realistic cost estimates (production 2026-10-06: a four-week, 3-a-day automation's 84-post month hit "Output hit max_tokens (8000)", was left with untitled posts, and advance-automations threw "Some posts have no topic yet" every 5 minutes with zero projects and no word to the owner; the month was also capped "cost_cap" far below the real limit). (1) The month-plan writer (services/content-plan-draft.ts writeTopics) writes PLAN_BATCH_SIZE = 10 posts per Claude call (MAX_PLANNING_OUTPUT_TOKENS 16 000 ÷ PLAN_ITEM_OUTPUT_TOKENS 1 600, a worst-case non-Latin post), each call with the planning cap and its slots numbered from 1, up to 3 batches at once, each wave told every title written so far; a title that repeats one already planned is rewritten in the second pass (PLAN_DRAFT_ROUNDS 2), a failed batch leaves only its own posts unwritten and is retried in that pass; writes only fill posts still untitled, so a retried job is idempotent and writes only what is missing; the plan becomes DRAFT only when every post has a topic, otherwise the job retries (retryable draft_incomplete) and its last attempt leaves a DRAFT with draftError. (2) The automation runner drafts a period's missing posts again (new run, plan metadata.redraftAttempts) instead of calling generatePlan, also when a DRAFTING period's job was lost (stale after 1 h); after MAX_REDRAFT_ATTEMPTS = 3 the automation is PAUSED with draft_failed + the paused notification (pause reason in 11 locales); resuming resets the attempts. The stuck production plan (DRAFT with untitled posts) is drafted again on the first tick after deploy. (3) typicalItemCostPence / keepCheapestFirst / the automation estimate price each FORMAT (TYPICAL_FORMAT_COST_PENCE, production measurements 2026-10-06: carousel 5p, wall of text 15p, slideshow 30p, hook + demo 50p; AI video and UGC keep the catalogue's typical short-video cost); capItems uses an item's format (month plans map VIDEO → AI video, SLIDESHOW → slideshow, CAROUSEL → carousel); maxItemCostPence stays the per-project budget cap. Tests: content-plan-draft.test.ts (84 posts → 9 batches ≤ 10, numbered from 1, all at 16 000 tokens, no repeated titles; a failing batch retried / reported missing), allowance.test.ts (format costs, an 84-post cheap month under £100 with £36 spent, cap still enforced), test/db/p23-month-batches.test.ts (four-week month drafted in batches, a retried job writes only the missing posts, a broken period heals on the next tick and activates, 3 failures → PAUSED draft_failed + one notification and no more jobs, resume resets, a lost DRAFTING job is queued again).
[2026-10-06] [23.6] Capacity under load (production 2026-10-06: a full-month automation started 41 posts at once; carousels that take ~26 s waited a median 17 min behind 4 orchestration slots; compose-video held a slot through every Shotstack render, p50 49 s; 100 users starting a month ≈ 44 h to drain). (1) **Rolling generation** (content-plans/rolling.ts): month plans and automation periods generate a post only within STUDIO_PLAN_LEAD_HOURS (default 72 h) of its slot, plus the plan's first STUDIO_PLAN_IMMEDIATE_ITEMS (default 3) at normal queue priority so the owner sees results at once; a post closer than twice its expected generation time (carousel 10 min … UGC 60 min) starts at once, first, at normal priority and outside the per-plan concurrency (late-start safety). The every-minute month-plan runner is the periodic tick (it now also runs for SCHEDULED plans with waiting posts); a plan becomes SCHEDULED (summary email, waiting posts counted as scheduled) once everything due now is made. DECISION: the allowance is still reserved for every post at approval ("Generate and schedule"), as before, so an approved month can never run out half way; waiting posts keep DRAFT projects. Plan API items gain createsAt; the plan and automation calendars show "Scheduled to be created on <date>" (11 locales) and no longer count waiting posts as being made. (2) **Asynchronous renders** (pipeline/render-async.ts, render-state.ts, queue/workers/poll-render.ts): compose-video builds every variant's edit, SUBMITS each Shotstack render (submitProvider: same routing, failover, rate windows, 20.29 slots — now released by lease id when the render ends — kill switch and tracked cost reservation as runProvider), records it in metadata.pendingRenders and ends; a delayed poll-render chain (20 s with callbacks, 5 s otherwise) or the Shotstack callback (which now promotes the delayed poll, BullMQ Job#promote, or adds one wake poll per provider job) claims the run (one poller at a time, lease 10 min), polls with pollTracked (cost settled once), stores / masters / records each finished render in one transaction as soon as it is seen, and moves the run to the quality gate once every variant is recorded (compare-and-set: the gate is enqueued once). Failed or timed-out renders (provider timeout unchanged, cancelled at the provider) are re-submitted by compose-video with the 3.9 backoff, at most 5 times, then the run fails composition_failed; a dead poll chain is restarted by the stuck re-drive. DECISION (multi-format): every variant is submitted together and each is recorded as it finishes; the quality gate starts when all are recorded (one gate per run, as before). The obvious seam for a renderer that returns the file at once (local FFmpeg) is inishRender. (3) **Lanes**: new studio-render queue (compose-video, poll-render, render-carousel; WORKER_CONCURRENCY_RENDER, default 3); a worker that runs orchestration also runs render unless STUDIO_RENDER_LANE=separate (VPS compose default list updated; Render.com orchestration services pick it up the same way); the month-plan, automation and Blitz runners run at HIGH priority. (4) **Redis**: completed jobs kept 6 h / 1 000 per queue (was 24 h / 5 000), failed jobs the newest 5 000 per queue (was unbounded); 
oeviction already set and CI-checked; recommended for the 3.8 GB server REDIS_MAXMEMORY=256mb / REDIS_MEM_LIMIT=384m (runbooks/vps-deploy.md). (5) **Capacity model** (load-test/capacity-*.ts, 
px tsx scripts/load-test/capacity-model.ts, no Redis needed): 100 organisations × 28 days × 3/day — initial backlog 32.7 h → 55 min, first post per organisation p95 97 → 17 min, 0 late posts, runner wait max 49 s → 7 s (runbooks/load-testing.md). Tests: rolling selection / late-start / settings / createsAt, runner tick on PGlite, calendar labels, async compose (submit + return, delayed polls, callback promote, duplicate polls and wakes settle once, timeout, multi-format) on PGlite, callback route wake, lease release, lanes / priorities / retention, inline delays, the capacity scenario.[2026-10-06] [23.4] Month plans in batches + self-healing automations + realistic cost estimates (production 2026-10-06: a four-week, 3-a-day automation's 84-post month hit "Output hit max_tokens (8000)", was left with untitled posts, and advance-automations threw "Some posts have no topic yet" every 5 minutes with zero projects and no word to the owner; the month was also capped "cost_cap" far below the real limit). (1) The month-plan writer (services/content-plan-draft.ts writeTopics) writes PLAN_BATCH_SIZE = 10 posts per Claude call (MAX_PLANNING_OUTPUT_TOKENS 16 000 ÷ PLAN_ITEM_OUTPUT_TOKENS 1 600, a worst-case non-Latin post), each call with the planning cap and its slots numbered from 1, up to 3 batches at once, each wave told every title written so far; a title that repeats one already planned is rewritten in the second pass (PLAN_DRAFT_ROUNDS 2), a failed batch leaves only its own posts unwritten and is retried in that pass; writes only fill posts still untitled, so a retried job is idempotent and writes only what is missing; the plan becomes DRAFT only when every post has a topic, otherwise the job retries (retryable draft_incomplete) and its last attempt leaves a DRAFT with draftError. (2) The automation runner drafts a period's missing posts again (new run, plan metadata.redraftAttempts) instead of calling generatePlan, also when a DRAFTING period's job was lost (stale after 1 h); after MAX_REDRAFT_ATTEMPTS = 3 the automation is PAUSED with draft_failed + the paused notification (pause reason in 11 locales); resuming resets the attempts. The stuck production plan (DRAFT with untitled posts) is drafted again on the first tick after deploy. (3) typicalItemCostPence / keepCheapestFirst / the automation estimate price each FORMAT (TYPICAL_FORMAT_COST_PENCE, production measurements 2026-10-06: carousel 5p, wall of text 15p, slideshow 30p, hook + demo 50p; AI video and UGC keep the catalogue's typical short-video cost); capItems uses an item's format (month plans map VIDEO → AI video, SLIDESHOW → slideshow, CAROUSEL → carousel); maxItemCostPence stays the per-project budget cap. [2026-10-06] [23.2] AI pipeline speed and reliability (operator request 2026-10-06; production last 3 days: Claude p50 6.4 s / p90 18.5 s and 66–86 s per AI or UGC video on one Sonnet model, AssemblyAI 17–43 s per AI video mostly transcribing our own narration, one ElevenLabs `concurrent_limit_exceeded` at the plan's 5, 9 HeyGen `insufficient_credits`). (1) Per-task Claude models (`providers/text-tasks.ts`): every call site names its task; light tasks (script safety, post captions + hashtags, hook line, wall text, Blitz angle suggestions, the UGC clip burned-in-text vision check) run on `ANTHROPIC_LIGHT_MODEL`, default Claude Haiku 4.5 `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` ($1 / $5 per MTok, platform.claude.com/docs/en/about-claude/pricing and models/overview, read 2026-10-06; price row added under ID and alias in `providers/anthropic-models.ts`); ideation, scripts, Blitz cards, month plans, carousel threads, slideshow text, website and library analysis stay on `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`. `ANTHROPIC_TASK_MODELS="task=model,…"` overrides one task, `ANTHROPIC_LIGHT_MODEL=off` puts everything back on the planning model; unknown tasks / unpriced models fail the worker at start and in the go-live env check. Cost and the router's budget estimate are priced at the model that ran; kill switch and budgets unchanged. No Claude call writes overlay labels (overlays/suggest.ts is deterministic), so none moved. (2) Parallel Claude calls in plan-project: the reads before ideation run together; ideation writes the brief only (no socialPosts) and the post copy is a separate light call from the brief that runs while Layer 2 writes the scripts (never fails the plan; Publish falls back to the hook as before); each script's safety check starts as soon as that script is written and the verdicts combine to the most severe (`scriptsWithSafety` / `combineScriptSafety`). Order kept where data depends; retries re-plan as before. (3) Narration word timings from ElevenLabs "Create speech with timing" (POST /v1/text-to-speech/{voice_id}/with-timestamps, elevenlabs.io/docs/api-reference/text-to-speech/convert-with-timestamps, read 2026-10-06; only `audio_base64` and `alignment.characters / character_start_times_seconds / character_end_times_seconds` are read): the character alignment becomes word timings (`pipeline/tts-alignment.ts`: whitespace words, punctuation kept on its word, apostrophes inside words, CJK one word per character, rejected unless the characters spell the narration text and times run forward) stored as `metadata.alignedWords`; `ensureWordTiming` uses them (re-spelt to the script as before) and only transcribes audio without them (UGC actor clips, uploads, a missing or bad alignment). `ELEVENLABS_WORD_TIMINGS=off` restores the plain endpoint. (4) ElevenLabs speech + music share one Redis-backed in-flight pool (20.29 limiter, new `CONCURRENCY_POOLS`) defaulting to the plan's 5 (org share 3; `STUDIO_PROVIDER_CONCURRENCY_ELEVENLABS`): the 6th call is deferred (no attempt spent) instead of failing; any provider can be capped the same way. (5) Confirmed the 20.11 account hold applies to HeyGen `insufficient_credits` on both the submit and the poll path (provider_jobs FAILED + breaker `tripAccount`, router skips it as `circuit_open`); production's 9 failures were 15-minute holds lapsing, so a credit problem now holds a provider for an hour (`ACCOUNT_CREDIT_HOLD_MS`; a stated resume time still wins, a success clears it). (6) Operator decision 2026-10-06 ("HeyGen should be used as a back-up; we have Seedance and other models that are better and cost less"): an AI_AVATAR presenter is made by the actor route first (`ugc/presenter.ts`; Veo 3.1 Fast `actor_video`, then Kling with native audio, the UGC_ACTOR request shape) speaking the shot's line natively, with the business's default READY creator portrait (22.3) or a one-off presenter portrait made once per project (21.4a claim, stored at `metadata.presenter.actorImage` so the project never becomes UGC); no TTS for that shot, captions from the clip speech (AssemblyAI, re-spelt), burned-in-text guard, platform AI labels, budgets / caps / kill switch / cost tracking unchanged. HeyGen then D-ID (`presenterRoute`; HeyGen before D-ID on every tier now) are the back-up, used when the actor route cannot make an equivalent shot (non-English line, line too long for the shot, shot outside 4–8 s, real-person scene, HeyGen chosen on regenerate) or no actor provider is available (account hold, breaker, kill switch, refusal); HeyGen still degrades to narrated B-roll (20.19). A brand custom HeyGen avatar would keep HeyGen first (no such avatar is stored yet). The planner offers AI_AVATAR whenever an actor or avatar provider exists and writes presenters as 4–8 s creator-style lines; UGC rules unchanged; AI_CLIP keeps Seedance first. Expected savings from the measured numbers: AI video (BRIEF, p50 431 s) ≈ 15–20 s (ideation without the copy ≈ 4–7 s, safety on Haiku ≈ 3–5 s, the post copy hidden behind Layer 2, narration transcription off each shot's path ≈ 6–11 s p50–p90; 17–43 s of AssemblyAI time and ≈ 10p a video saved); UGC (p50 230 s) ≈ 10–15 s (ideation + safety ≈ 7–12 s, clip-text check on Haiku ≈ 3 s per actor shot); hook + demo / wall of text ≈ 6–9 s (hook line or block + safety on Haiku); carousels, slideshows, uploads ≈ 3–5 s (safety); Publish "Suggest captions" and Blitz angles answer faster; light calls cost half (Haiku vs Sonnet 5 list price). Tests: task → model map, per-task cost and estimates, call-site tasks, env validators, alignment → words (punctuation, multi-space, apostrophes, CJK, mismatch), ElevenLabs timed endpoint, AssemblyAI skipped with alignment and used without, 5-slot ElevenLabs pool (6th deferred, music shares it, org share), parallel scripts + per-script safety, post copy started before Layer 2 is awaited and never failing the plan, credit hold 1 h, presenter route order and skip rules, and on Postgres (PGlite): presenter via Veo with the creator portrait, one-off presenter portrait, HeyGen only when Veo is out of credits or held, golden captions / fallback / UGC journeys. Cost baseline updated (budgeted 30 s short 241p → 232p at 0.79). OPERATOR NOTE: at list prices a presenter shot costs more on Veo 3.1 Fast (8 s with a reference portrait × $0.10 = $0.80 ≈ 60p) than on HeyGen (6 s × $0.05 = $0.30 ≈ 23p + narration) — the decision is implemented as asked; `STUDIO_PROVIDER_CONCURRENCY_*` / regenerate-on-HeyGen remain available. NOT VERIFIED: no live call to the with-timestamps endpoint, Haiku, or a Veo presenter clip from this machine (no keys); production timing gains are estimates from the 2026-10-06 measurements.
[2026-10-06] [23.3] Cheap posts count ¼ (operator decision 2026-10-06): a carousel, slideshow, wall of text or hook + demo uses a quarter of a video of the allowance and of HD video packs; an AI video still uses 1 and a UGC actor video 2. The per-channel plan's 8 HD videos a channel a month give up to 32 quick posts (weekly 2 videos = 8 quick posts a week; yearly 8 a month released = 32). Applies to every customer, plan, trial and interval; cost caps are unchanged (these posts cost 3–41p each on production, so a month of 3 a day fits). DECISION (representation): allowance is counted in integer QUARTERS of a video wherever it is added up or stored (`billing/allowance-units.ts`: quick post 1, video 4, UGC 8; 8 videos = 32 quarters); limits stay in videos where they are configured (catalogue, entitlements, env) and are converted at the edge; customers see videos as a quarter number (5.5 of 8; a quarter is exact in floating point). `ugc/allowance.ts allowanceQuartersOf(project)` replaces `allowanceUnitsOf` (source type, or the stored carousel / wallOfText / hookDemo document); `plan-quotas.ts monthlyQuarterUsage` (was monthlyVideoUsage); `generateViolations` compares quarters; the usage meter adds `usedQuarters` / `limitQuarters`; Blitz keeps and month-plan / automation slot reservations go through the same locked check; `capItems` takes items with their quarters and automations keep slots by quarters (`blitz/allocate.ts FORMAT_ALLOWANCE_QUARTERS`). Packs: expand-only, idempotent migration `20261011010000_quick_post_quarters` adds `usage_credits.quantityQuarters / remainingQuarters` (backfilled ×4, then NOT NULL) and `usage_credit_uses.quarters` (default 4; a UGC project's earlier use backfilled to 8); consumption, release (exactly the use's quarters back) and refunds count quarters; `quantity` / `remaining` stay as legacy video values written at purchase. Customer copy in 11 locales: the usage banner, Your plan, the Plan my month allowance panel and the upgrade dialog state that carousels, slideshows, wall of text and hook + demo count as ¼; the plan picker (/pricing and Your plan) adds "or up to N quick posts"; /pricing hero, an included line and a FAQ entry; the Blitz card says "Uses ¼ of a video"; the hook + demo disclosure says ¼. Terms 6.1 / 6.3 / 6.5, FILL-IN and runbooks/billing-stripe.md updated (no generated legal / go-live HTML changes: go-live.md is unchanged). Demo: the default state is 19.5 of 24 (two carousels), demo generations of quick posts count ¼, a What's new entry. Tests: quarters per format, quarter arithmetic and meters, capItems with mixed kinds and credit headroom, Postgres (monthly 32 quick posts, mixed formats, weekly ISO-week and yearly windows, packs consume / release / refund in quarters, UGC 8, the migration backfill run twice in a rolled-back transaction), a Blitz keep counts 1 quarter, automation slots kept by quarters, a month plan of slideshows, usage banner and pricing copy.
