[2026-10-03] [20.25] Cheaper videos (operator decision 2026-10-03): provider cost per video cut so the plans stay profitable without changing prices or allowances. QA run 3 (2026-10-02, a full 30 s TikTok) cost £3.33: Veo 8 clips 240p, Shotstack 60p, AssemblyAI 10 jobs 10p, ElevenLabs TTS 10 jobs 10p, Anthropic 7p, music 6p, against the catalogue's typical short cost of BASIC 90p / STANDARD 160p / PLUS 240p. New `pipeline/clip-budget.ts`: an AI clip budget per tier and length (stated in the Layer 2 prompt and enforced after parsing in plan-project and regenerate-script), AI clips of at most 4 s, 480p on BASIC; budget-converted shots get the business's image, then stock, then a motion-graphics card; every IMAGE_STILL tries stock before a paid generation; stills alternate zoomIn / zoomOut; clips and stills fill the frame with Shotstack `fit: crop` (aspect kept) instead of `cover` (stretched); Shotstack priced at its list price; new `cost/video-estimate.ts` cost model. Typical 30 s short now (list prices, USD→GBP 0.75 as in production, Seedance first): BASIC 76p, STANDARD 141p, PLUS 187p (at the cost harness's 0.79: 79p / 145p / 193p). Long form: STANDARD 3 min 805p (≤ 900p), PLUS 6 min 1,637p (≤ 1,800p). Tests: `pipeline/clip-budget.test.ts` (budgets, enforcement and priorities, durations, prompt and schema), `cost/video-estimate.test.ts` (per-tier cost ≤ catalogue at 0.75 and 0.79, long form, project budget, monthly caps), `pipeline/edl-stills.test.ts`, `pipeline/still-image.test.ts`, Seedance 480p / router BASIC / Shotstack price tests, golden `test/golden/clip-budget.test.ts` (a STANDARD 30 s short whose script makes every shot an AI clip → 4 AI clips at ≤ 4 s and 720p, images or cards for the rest, no generated image, quality gate PASSED, READY_FOR_REVIEW; BASIC → 480p; a model-chosen still uses stock first), cost-regression journeys `budgeted-30s-short-*` and a new baseline.
- DECISION: AI clip budget = max(2, round(first 60 s ÷ tier rate + the rest ÷ max(tier rate, 8 s))); tier rate BASIC 10 s, STANDARD 7 s, PLUS / ENTERPRISE 5 s. A 30 s short gets BASIC 3, STANDARD 4, PLUS 6 AI clips (operator range BASIC 3, STANDARD 4–5, PLUS 6–8: STANDARD 4 and PLUS 6 keep a margin, since 5 / 8 clips would put STANDARD at ~164p and PLUS at ~233p at 0.75); 15 s: 2 / 2 / 3; STANDARD 3 min: 24; PLUS 6 min: 50. AI_AVATAR shots count towards it.
- DECISION: enforcement. The script schema gains an optional `beat` (hook | demo | cta | other; never persisted). Extra AI_CLIP / AI_AVATAR shots become IMAGE_STILL shots marked `providerRouting.clipBudget { convertedFrom, budget }` (narration and on-screen text kept). Kept first: the hook (the model's beat, else the first shot), the call to action (else the last shot), the demo, then the shot farthest from those already kept. A regenerated script's pinned AI shots spend the budget first. AI_CLIP shots longer than 4 s are cut to 4 s and the freed time goes to the other shots, unless they cannot take it (the ordinary 2–10 s bounds then stay) or a TEMPLATE fixes the lengths.
- DECISION: what a converted shot shows (operator order). Layer 3 (`generate-asset.ts budgetStill`) never pays for it: the business's image library (pgvector match ≥ 0.30), then a storable stock image (Pexels, Storyblocks, Pixabay), else the shot becomes MOTION_GRAPHICS (TEXT_CARD when Shotstack is not the composer) drawn from its on-screen text or narration, recorded as `visual { providerId: 'composer', chosenBy: 'clip_budget_card' }`. Stock problems (no key, search errors) are a miss, never a failed shot.
- DECISION: every IMAGE_STILL (also the ones the model chooses) now tries a stock image after the library and before a paid generation (operator: "the business's own images, then stock"); a regenerated still skips library and stock as before and keeps the refusal → stock fallback. Generated stills (4p) become the exception.
- DECISION: resolution and length. AI clips are kept to 4 s, so each is one provider-minimum clip (Seedance 4 s, Kling 3 s, Veo 4 s; the adapters already round a shot up to the minimum and the composer trims). New optional `resolution` (`480p` | `720p`) on text/image-to-video requests: BASIC 480p, other tiers 720p (`AI_CLIP_RESOLUTION`). Seedance reads it (480p frames from the create-task pixel table, read 2026-10-03: 2.0 series 9:16 496 × 864, 2.5 9:16 480 × 854; same price per token, so a 4 s 9:16 clip is 11p instead of 23p) and prices it; Kling and Veo have no 480p and keep 720p. A 480p clip is fingerprinted apart (`seed: resolution:480p`) so it is never reused for a 720p shot.
- DECISION: Seedance model. Every tier now uses 2.0 mini; 2.5 renders only shots over 15 s (supersedes the 20.23 "2.5 on PLUS / ENTERPRISE"). 2.5 at 720p is $0.231/s (70p per 4 s clip): PLUS's 6 clips alone would cost 420p (typical 240p) and any tier's short would pass the 350p default project budget (the guard pauses a run at 90%). `SEEDANCE_MODEL` can still raise every tier's model.
- DECISION: BASIC AI_CLIP routing seedance → kling → veo → fal → replicate (was fal → replicate, which have no adapters: BASIC AI clips had no provider at all). Runway and Luma stay off BASIC.
- DECISION: Shotstack estimate at its list price (shotstack.io/pricing, read 2026-10-03): $0.30 per rendered minute pay-as-you-go ($0.20 on a subscription), one credit per minute "regardless of resolution", rounded down to the second. The spec 6.5 figure (£0.02/s, 60p per 30 s short) was about 5× list and was what Studio recorded (Shotstack reports no per-render cost). A 30 s render is now 12p. `ShotstackAdapter` takes `usdToGbpRate`.
- DECISION: `TYPICAL_COST_PENCE_PER_VIDEO` unchanged (BASIC 90 / STANDARD 160 / PLUS 240 short, 900 / 1,800 long): the new plan fits under it with a margin, and lowering it would change the margins the price list was set from. The default project budget becomes per tier (next DECISION). Monthly caps hold the full allowance at the typical cost (BASIC 20 × 76p = 1,520p ≤ 2,000p; STANDARD 40 × 141p = 5,640p + one long ≤ 7,300p; PLUS 80 × 187p = 14,960p + longs ≤ 26,400p).
- DECISION (coordinator, production finding 2026-10-03: a STANDARD video paused at "90% of its budget (£3.15 of £3.50)"): the default per-project budget now follows the organisation's plan tier, at least 2.5 × the catalogue's typical cost per video and never under the operator's £3.50 / £30 (`cost/project-budget.ts` shortFormBudgetPence / longFormBudgetPence): short BASIC 350p, STANDARD 400p, PLUS and ENTERPRISE 600p; long STANDARD 3,000p, PLUS and ENTERPRISE 4,500p; slideshows 150p. Applied at creation and duplication (`toPlanTier(tenant.organisation.planTier)`), shown on Create (placeholder and hint take the plan tier; no new strings), and used as a month plan's "up to" per video. A typical short is now under half its budget on every tier, and even on the Veo failover under the 90% pause (tested). Explicit budgets and existing projects are unchanged.
- DECISION (stock sources after merging main's 20.16): budget stills and stills use main's stock order (Pexels → Storyblocks → Pixabay, then Unsplash) through `deps.scan.stock()`, but only storable hits: Pixabay is copied into our storage and used; Unsplash's terms require hotlinking and forbid stored copies, while video shots, the composer, export and retention all assume a stored asset, so Unsplash hits are skipped for video shots (slideshows keep hotlinking them) and such a shot falls through to a motion-graphics card. Follow-up if wanted: hotlinked video-shot assets (composer uses `publicUrl`, report use to download_location).
- DECISION (coordinator 2026-10-03): video EDL clips and stills use `fit: 'crop'` (`edl.ts MEDIA_FIT`). Shotstack's Clip docs (https://shotstack.io/docs/api/#tocs_clip, read 2026-10-03): "crop (default) - scale the asset to fill the viewport while maintaining the aspect ratio. The asset will be cropped if it exceeds the bounds of the viewport"; "cover - stretch the asset to fill the viewport without maintaining the aspect ratio". `cover` (used since Phase 3) stretched a 9:16 clip in a 16:9 render, a 3:4 Seedance clip in 4:5, or a landscape stock photo in 9:16. Tests for 9:16, 16:9, 1:1 and 4:5 renders with mixed clips, avatar, upload and stills. The slideshow EDL is changed in 20.26.
- FINDING (no change): AssemblyAI runs once per shot because 13.6 word timing is per narration file (each shot is voiced separately and fitted in its own generate-asset job: narration-fit trims at a word boundary; the karaoke captions and caption_sync read per-shot words). One transcription of the whole narration would need the shots' audio joined before Layer 4 finishes. Its real cost is about $0.002 per 30 s; the recorded 10p is ten 1p round-ups (usdToPence rounds each call up). The same holds for ElevenLabs TTS (about 3p of characters, recorded 10p). Kept: per-shot timing needs it. Option for later: ElevenLabs `with-timestamps` TTS returns character timings and would remove the AssemblyAI calls.
- FINDING (Shotstack output options, no change): output is 1080 × 1920 mp4 at 30 fps (`edl.ts OUTPUT_RESOLUTION` / render presets). Shotstack bills the same per minute at any resolution up to 1080p, so a lower resolution or preview quality saves nothing; the `stage` environment is free but watermarked (QA only). Savings available: a Shotstack subscription ($0.20/min, −33%) and avoiding re-renders (the 15.B6 composition cache already re-points identical edits).
- RISK: the budgets assume Seedance is configured (`BYTEPLUS_API_KEY`). If Kling serves the clips a 30 s short is BASIC 121p / STANDARD 153p / PLUS 205p; on Veo 3.1 Fast 133p / 169p / 229p (BASIC is over 90p either way, since neither offers 480p).
- RISK (quality): converted shots with no library or stock match are motion-graphics cards; a card shows the shot's on-screen text (or narration) while the suggested headline overlay may show the same words (as for a model-chosen MOTION_GRAPHICS shot today). Stock images are matched on the scene description only, and shots after the first stock hit may reuse that image from the library (similarity ≥ 0.30).

[2026-10-03] [20.26] Slideshows came out black and failed the quality gate (production 2026-10-03; project cmurp3szx001zql068q4fkj0f "3 Steps to Nail Your Meeting Opener": six TEXT_CARD slides, `imageAssetId` and `backgroundColor` NULL, 2.5 s each; black_frames 0.0–15.0 s on x, youtube, facebook, facebook_feed, linkedin_video and instagram_feed, 2.5–15.0 s on another project's instagram_reel; youtube_short passed). Root causes: (1) `slideshow/edl.ts` drew a slide with no colour of its own on the brand background or `#000000`, and set the timeline background to the same (20.22 had fixed video cards only); (2) its font sizes were fractions of the frame HEIGHT, so 16:9 and 1:1 frames (1080 px high) drew text at 56 % of the 9:16 size (1920 px high): white text on black covered under 2 % of a landscape/square frame, and blackdetect (pic_th 0.98) reads a frame as black when 98 % of its pixels are, while the larger 9:16 text kept most portrait frames above that line (most likely why the other reel went black from 2.5 s, after its longer hook; that project was not inspected); no clip was off-canvas or missing per aspect (HtmlAsset boxes were full-frame, `position: center`); (3) every slide was a TEXT_CARD because `services/content-plan-run.ts` built a month-plan slideshow's hook, points and CTA all as TEXT_CARD, and image population (library → AI) only ran from the manual auto-populate button and only for image slide types. Fixes: the slideshow EDL uses the 20.22 backdrop rule (`pipeline/edl-backdrop.ts`: brand background if luma ≥ 0.2, else lifted, else slate #3A4150) for cards, image-less slides and the timeline, keeps a colour the user picked, picks card text for WCAG AA contrast, sizes text from the short side (`textBase`, 1920 at 1080p on every aspect, so all aspects draw the 9:16 sizes) with text boxes at least as tall as their lines, falls back to a backdrop card when an image/video is missing, and draws images with `fit: crop` (Shotstack: `cover` "stretch[es] the asset … without maintaining the aspect ratio"). Month-plan points are IMAGE_KENBURNS slides (text as the caption, `imageQuery` = the point, Ken Burns moves cycled; hook and CTA stay brand text cards). `plan-slideshow` now fills imageless image slides before checking them (`slideshow/populate.ts fillSlideImages`): the business library → stock on demand (`slideshow/slide-images.ts`, `stockSourcesFromEnv` order: Pixabay, then Unsplash; stored exactly as the 20.16 stock layer through the new shared `images/library.ts storeStockHit`: Pixabay copied and credited, Unsplash hotlinked with download tracking on use; 3 hits per slide search, Pixabay's 24 h search cache) → an AI image within the existing per-run (5), per-org-day (100) and monthly business caps → a TEXT_CARD on the backdrop; library and generation outages are misses there. Manual auto-populate gains the free stock step and otherwise behaves as before. Profile saves top up a thin library (below). Tests: `slideshow/edl-aspects.test.ts` (9:16, 16:9, 1:1, 4:5: no black backdrop or gap, boxes inside the canvas at a valid position, 96 px card text everywhere fitting its box, AA contrast, user colour kept, image crop + caption band, missing image → backdrop), `slide-images.test.ts`, 7 new `fillSlideImages` cases (empty library → stock → images; library first; stock fails → AI; stock + AI fail → text card; budget spent → text card; manual populate unchanged; no empty text card), `storeStockHit` / `perQuery` library cases, `image-library-auto.test.ts`, PGlite API test `test/api/p20-auto-stock.test.ts`, and the golden `test/golden/slideshow-visuals.test.ts`: the production slideshow and a photo slideshow reach READY_FOR_REVIEW on youtube_short, instagram_reel, youtube, linkedin_video, facebook_feed, x (1:1) and instagram_feed (4:5) with a stub media inspector that derives blackdetect from the edit Shotstack received (on the old EDL it reports black 0.0–15.0 s on every format, as in production); stock down → AI images; stock and AI down → text cards. Shotstack docs cited in `slideshow/edl.ts` (https://shotstack.io/docs/api/, read 2026-10-03).
- DECISION: automatic stock refresh when a business profile is saved (PATCH business-profile, fields that change which photos suit the business), not when a business is created: a new business has a name and no profile, so there is nothing to search with, and the website scan that creates the profile already runs the stock layer. Cheap and rate-limited: only while the library has fewer than 20 images, the first 3 profile queries × 6 images, at most once per business a day (any stock search of the business in the last 24 h counts), inside the organisation's hourly refresh cap shared with Refresh stock, and through Pixabay's 24 h search cache. It never fails the save; the response says why it did or did not run (`stockRefresh`: queued, recent, library_full, rate_limited, not_configured, no_queries, not_relevant, failed).
- DECISION: when no image can be found at generation time, an image slide with text becomes a TEXT_CARD (persisted, `backgroundColor` NULL so it takes the brand backdrop, duration clamped to the text-card range) rather than failing the run with "needs an image"; an image slide without text still fails as before. Manual auto-populate keeps the old behaviour (the slide stays unfilled for the user to pick an image).
- DECISION: the month-plan photo slides keep the 2.5 s of the text cards they replace (the Ken Burns minimum), so plan slideshows keep their 15 s length and quota.
- FINDING (not changed, video storyboard belongs to 20.25): `pipeline/edl.ts` also draws video IMAGE/clip assets with `fit: 'cover'`, which Shotstack documents as stretching without keeping the aspect ratio. Fixed in 20.25 (`fit: 'crop'`, merged after this entry).
[2026-10-03] [20.16] Pixabay merged up to main (PR #64 had never been merged, so production answered "Stock photos aren't set up yet" (QA-6 `stock_not_configured` 501) with `PIXABAY_API_KEY` set). Conflicts resolved keeping both sides: env-file validators (Veo / Seedance / Kling from main + `PIXABAY_API_KEY`; Hive checks dropped with 20.21; `stockChecks` kept), BACKLOG / PROGRESS, go-live (Pixabay is now §11.5 "Stock images: Pixabay (free) and Unsplash", after Veo 11.2, Seedance 11.3, Kling 11.4; docs-site regenerated). Unsplash is approved for production: `UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY` added to `deploy/vps/.env.example` (it was missing there) and the vps-deploy table, documented in `.env.example` and go-live 11.5; with both keys Pixabay is a primary and Unsplash the fallback (searched only when the primaries found nothing for a query; hotlinked and use-reported as before). New PGlite API tests (`test/api/qa6-business-connections.test.ts`): through the real `stockSourcesFromEnv` wiring, Refresh stock answers 202 (not 501) with only `PIXABAY_API_KEY`, with Pixabay + Unsplash, and with only Unsplash.
[2026-10-01] [20.16] Pixabay as a free stock image source (Pexels no longer issues free API keys; Unsplash approval takes 5-10 working days). `createPixabaySource` in `src/lib/studio/images/stock.ts`, enabled by `PIXABAY_API_KEY`, built from https://pixabay.com/api/docs/ (read 2026-10-01, cited in code): GET https://pixabay.com/api/ with `key`, `q` (trimmed to the documented 100 characters), `image_type=photo`, `safesearch=true`, `per_page` clamped to 3-200, orientation landscape/portrait mapped to horizontal/vertical (square = no filter), optional `min_width` (new optional `StockSearch.minWidth`). Hits map to `largeImageURL` (1280 px) by default or `webformatURL` (640 px) when the caller needs at most 640 px, with dimensions scaled to that size; `tags` as alt text; attribution = Pixabay `user` + profile URL `https://pixabay.com/users/{user}-{id}/`; `pageURL` as the page; `storable: true`, so every chosen image is downloaded into our storage through the existing `ingestImage` path (never hotlinked; `previewURL` is never used). 24 h search cache (`images/stock-cache.ts`): Redis (`studio:stockcache:<provider>:<sha256 of the key-less params>`, SET PX 24 h, the existing `createBreakerRedisClient`) when REDIS_URL is set, else an in-process LRU (500 entries); Redis errors fall back to the LRU (warned once a minute); failed requests are not cached; the API key is never part of the key. Errors use the shared stock mapping: 429 → `rate_limited` (retryable), 401/403 → `auth`, 5xx → `provider_unavailable`; a 400 whose text names the API key → `auth` (20.11 account class); provider text is classified, never echoed. `buildStockLayer` already moves on to the next source on any error. Licence note (`stockLicenceNote`, now also used by the still-image refusal fallback, which previously stored no credit): `Pixabay Content License (https://pixabay.com/service/license-summary/); Photo by <user> (<profile>); Image from Pixabay: <pageURL>`, and `sourceUrl` = pageURL. UI: the image library (and its semantic search results) credits stock images on each tile and under the grid — "Images from Pixabay and Pexels" (`business.images.stockCredit`, 11 locales, review lists updated; provider names are brands, not translated). Config: `PIXABAY_API_KEY` in `.env.example` and `deploy/vps/.env.example` (OPTIONAL, where to get it); `setup:check` rejects a value with spaces (Pixabay documents no key format, so nothing stricter) and warns `CHECK stock images` when no stock key at all is set; `pixabay` added to the kill-switch provider ids (`system-flags.ts`, admin types, demo). "No stock image provider configured" now names PIXABAY_API_KEY first. Runbooks: go-live.md 11.2 "Stock images: Pixabay (free)" (docs-site/go-live.html regenerated) and the vps-deploy.md env table. Tests (fetch mocked, no real calls): request building, clamping, orientation, response mapping (large/web format, fallbacks, attribution), source ordering, 24 h cache hit and expiry, cache key without the API key and order-independent, corrupt entry and failed request not cached, 429/401/403/400-key classification, Pixabay hit stored via ingest (not the hotlink path) with the credit note, memory LRU / Redis / fail-open cache, env validation and the stock reminder, library panel credit. Results: images, setup, providers, slideshow, populate-slideshow, system-flags, admin, kill-switch, vps-compose, i18n suites green (images 118, business components 54, related 876 + 4 skipped); `tsc --noEmit`, eslint, prettier on changed files, `check-catalogues` and `check-physical-css --fail` pass.
- DECISION: order Pexels → Storyblocks → Pixabay as primaries, Unsplash fallback, as asked. Pixabay goes last among primaries so an operator with a Pexels or Storyblocks key keeps today's results; with only Pixabay configured (the expected case now) it is the first source searched.
- DECISION: Pixabay is not added to `providerKeysFromEnv` / the BYOC registry: that map feeds ProviderAdapters (Pexels there means the Pexels *video* adapter) and stock images are not adapters, so there is no adapter health to show; the setup check reminder and the kill-switch id cover the operator view.
- DECISION: the cache stores the raw `hits` array and remaps on read, so a later mapping change applies to cached entries; `webformatURL` is documented as valid for 24 hours, so a cached hit near the end of its 24 h may fail to download — that image is skipped (ingest error) and the next refresh refetches.
- DECISION: the credit is shown on stored copies in the library (Studio never shows live Pixabay search results), always visible under the grid and on each tile.
- UNCONFIRMED: Pixabay documents only the 429 error and no key format; the 400 "API key" → auth mapping is based on Pixabay's observed plain-text error, not its docs, and other 400s stay `invalid_request`.
[2026-10-02] [20.24] Kling 3.0 as an AI_CLIP provider (operator decision 2026-10-02). `src/lib/studio/providers/kling.ts` implements ProviderAdapter over Kling AI's official API (docs read 2026-10-02 on kling.ai/document-api, cited in the file; app.klingai.com/global/dev/document-api now 301-redirects there): `POST https://api-singapore.klingai.com/text-to-video/kling-3.0` `{ prompt, settings: { multi_shot: false, audio: "off", resolution, aspect_ratio, duration }, options: { watermark_info: { enabled: false } } }`, `POST /image-to-video/kling-3.0` with `contents: [{ type: "prompt", text }, { type: "first_frame", url }]` (no aspect_ratio: the frame decides), poll `GET /tasks?task_ids={id}` (status submitted | processing | succeeded | failed; video at `outputs[type=video].url`, cleared after 30 days, so Layer 3 copies it at once by plain download, no fetchOutput), health = `GET /account/costs` (free, QPS ≤ 1), no documented cancel (NotImplementedError). Auth: `Authorization: Bearer <KLING_API_KEY>` (current scheme), or the legacy `KLING_ACCESS_KEY` + `KLING_SECRET_KEY` signed per call into an HS256 JWT `{ iss: AK, exp: now+1800, nbf: now-5 }` (`signKlingJwt`, node:crypto, no new dependency); the API key wins when both are set, half a pair is a ConfigurationError. Shots map to whole seconds 3–15 (a 1–2 s shot renders 3 s and is trimmed; > 15 s skips Kling via `supportsRequest`), 16:9 / 9:16 / 1:1 (4:5 → 9:16), 720p default or 1080p (`KLING_RESOLUTION`), always silent and single-shot. Cost = seconds × silent rate (720p 0.6 units = $0.084/s, 1080p 0.8 units = $0.112/s at the $0.14 unit list price) in pence; at poll time the task's own `billing` (resource-pack units × $0.14, or a USD cash charge) is reported as `metadata.costPence`, CNY or unreadable bills keep the estimate. Errors map Kling's service codes to the 20.11/20.19 classes: 1000–1004, 1103 (no access to the model) and 1304 (IP allow-list) → auth; 1101 (arrears) and 1102 (resource pack used up / expired) → insufficient_credits; 1100 (abnormal account) → account_limit; 1200–1203 → invalid_request; 1300/1301 → content_policy; 1302/1303 (concurrency over the pack limit) → rate_limited (retry); 5000/5001 → provider_unavailable, 5002 → timeout; unknown codes fall back to the HTTP status; `isOutOfCreditMessage` upgrades input/unknown errors with out-of-credit wording (`withOutOfCredit`, as in veo.ts); a 200 whose envelope code ≠ 0 is an error too. Failed tasks: out-of-credit text → insufficient_credits, content-risk text → content_policy, else unknown (retryable). Wiring: default registry (`KLING_API_KEY` or the pair; BYOC id `kling` with an API key, a secondary key = the legacy pair), canary (`CANARY_KLING_API_KEY`, platform-canary.yml), staging gate `--live-providers` entry `kling` (one 3 s 720p silent clip, ≈ $0.25), kill switch (`kling` was already in PROVIDER_IDS / admin types / demo admin; test added), env vars in both .env.example files and the setup checker (`KLING_API_KEY` shape, the pair must be complete, `KLING_MODEL` = kling-3.0, `KLING_RESOLUTION` 720p|1080p, `KLING_BASE_URL` https origin), runbooks (go-live 11.3 step by step, provider-outage, vps-deploy table, SLO canary secrets), docs-site/go-live.html regenerated, demo tour/queues, sub-processor row (Kling AI Pte. Ltd., Singapore) with a LEGAL REVIEW comment. Tests: kling.test.ts (JWT with a fixed clock, credentials choice, request building, duration/aspect/resolution mapping, no audio, submit → submitted → processing → succeeded → URL, billing cost, failed task classes, every service code, out-of-credit wording, envelope errors, health, cancel, kill switch), registry, env checker, router order + failover (breakers, account exclusion, kill switch, 10 s shot skips Veo, 15/16 s limits, image-to-video), runProvider failover with the real adapter's 1102 → Veo.
- DECISION: routing. Operator order Seedance → Kling 3 → Veo 3.1 Fast → Runway → Luma on STANDARD, PLUS and ENTERPRISE (BASIC unchanged: fal → replicate). Merged with 20.23 (release 2026-10-02b): AI_CLIP is now seedance → kling → veo → runway → luma on every paid tier; Seedance chooses 2.0 mini or 2.5 by tier inside its adapter.
- DECISION: no audio. `settings.audio: "off"` on every request (silent 720p 0.6 units/s vs 0.9 with native audio; the composer mutes generated clips anyway). `multi_shot: false` (Kling's default is true).
- DECISION: `KLING_RESOLUTION` instead of the requested `KLING_MODE`. The current Kling 3.0 API has no std/pro `mode`, no `negative_prompt` and no `cfg_scale` (those were legacy `/v1/videos/*` parameters); resolution is the quality/price knob. 4k (3.0 units/s = $0.42/s) is not offered. `KLING_MODEL` accepts only `kling-3.0` (the only priced path; Kling 3.0 Turbo is billed "with native audio" only and Omni is a different API).
- DECISION: both credentials (coordinator request). The authentication page says "API Key authentication must be used to call Kling AI API" and labels AccessKey/SecretKey "only applicable to legacy version design standards"; we still accept the pair so an operator can paste whichever the console shows, preferring the API key.
- CONFIRMED (coordinator, 2026-10-02, free call): the production `KLING_API_KEY` answered `GET https://api-singapore.klingai.com/account/costs` with `Authorization: Bearer <key>` → 200 `{ code: 0, message: "SUCCEED" }`, so the base URL and Bearer API-key auth are confirmed.
- UNCONFIRMED (verify with `staging-gate --live-providers --only kling` or a real shot): (1) whether the kling-3.0 endpoints accept the legacy AK/SK JWT. (2) Whether output URLs ("hotlink protection format") download without credentials or a Referer; if not, add `fetchOutput`. (3) Failed-task message wording (only "content risk control" is documented). (4) Typical latency (not documented; 180 s used for deadlines). (5) Whether `billing.amount` for units is per task as we assume. (6) The API key format (only its shape is checked). (7) Kling AI Pte. Ltd.'s registered address (1 Raffles Place, Singapore per third-party sources), its DPA / SCCs and processing location: the sub-processor row needs legal review before `KLING_API_KEY` is set in production. (8) Image constraints (jpg/png, ≤ 50 MB, ≥ 300 px, aspect 1:2.5–2.5:1) are documented but not checked locally; Kling rejects a bad frame with 1201 (invalid_request).

[2026-10-02] [20.23] BytePlus ModelArk Seedance as the main text-to-video provider (operator decision 2026-10-02; routing approved by the operator the same day). `src/lib/studio/providers/seedance.ts` implements ProviderAdapter over the ModelArk video generation API (docs read 2026-10-02, pages last updated 2026-09-28, cited in the file): `POST https://ark.ap-southeast.bytepluses.com/api/v3/contents/generations/tasks` with `Authorization: Bearer <key>` (base URL and Bearer auth also verified live by the operator: the list call returned 200), body `{ model, content: [{ type: "text", text }, { type: "image_url", image_url: { url }, role: "first_frame" }?], ratio, duration, resolution: "720p", generate_audio: false, watermark: false, execution_expires_after: 3600, safety_identifier: sha256(organisationId) }` → `{ id }`; poll `GET …/tasks/{id}` (`queued` / `running` / `succeeded` / `failed` / `expired` / `cancelled`), video at `content.video_url` (pre-signed, valid 24 h; 2.5 links allow 100 downloads), billed tokens at `usage.completion_tokens` (reported as the actual `costPence`); cancel = `DELETE …/tasks/{id}`, which works only while `queued` (running → NotImplementedError, finished → no-op); health = `GET …/tasks?page_num=1&page_size=1` (unbilled). text_to_video + image_to_video (frame passed as its https URL). Models (list prices, USD per million tokens, 480p/720p, input without video): `dreamina-seedance-2-0-mini-260615` 3.5 (4–15 s, default), `dreamina-seedance-2-0-fast-260128` 5.6, `dreamina-seedance-2-0-260128` 7.0, `dreamina-seedance-2-5-260628` 10.7 (4–30 s). Tokens = seconds × width × height × 24 / 1024 (720p 16:9 = 21,600 a second: mini $0.0756/s, fast $0.121/s, 2.0 $0.151/s, 2.5 $0.231/s); a 4 s 9:16 mini clip ≈ $0.30, 5 s ≈ $0.38. Shots render at the next whole second (minimum 4 s; the composer trims); ratios 9:16 / 16:9 / 1:1 direct, 4:5 → 3:4 (composer "cover" crops); 2.5 image-to-video sends `ratio: "adaptive"` (the only value it accepts). Routing (`router.ts`): AI_CLIP on STANDARD, PLUS and ENTERPRISE = seedance → veo → runway → luma → kling; the tier picks the Seedance model: `runProvider` now adds `planTier` to the request (`ProviderRequestBase.planTier`, optional) and the adapter uses `SEEDANCE_MODEL` (2.0 mini) on STANDARD/BASIC and `SEEDANCE_LONG_MODEL` (2.5) on PLUS/ENTERPRISE and for any shot longer than the default model's 15 s; `supportsRequest()` skips shots longer than every configured model (31 s+, or 16 s+ when the long model is a 15 s one). 20.19's presenterless avatar fallback routes through AI_CLIP, so Seedance is its first candidate too. Errors (ModelArk error codes): AuthenticationError / InvalidAccountStatus / AccessDenied / OperationDenied.ServiceNotOpen / ModelNotOpen / InvalidEndpointOrModel.* → auth; AccountOverdueError / OperationDenied.ServiceOverdue / QuotaExceeded "free trial" → insufficient_credits; SetLimitExceeded / QuotaExceeded "5-hour/weekly/monthly" → account_limit; RateLimitExceeded.* / Model…RateLimitExceeded / RequestBurstTooFast / InflightBatchsizeExceeded / QuotaExceeded (queued tasks) → rate_limited; ServerOverloaded / InternalServiceError / InvalidEndpoint.ClosedEndpoint → provider_unavailable; every *SensitiveContentDetected* code (moderation, copyright `.PolicyViolation`, real faces `.PrivacyInformation`) → content_policy; InvalidParameter* / MissingParameter* / InvalidImageURL.* → invalid_request; unknown codes fall back to the HTTP status; out-of-credit or arrears wording (`isOutOfCreditMessage` + "overdue balance" / "in arrears") upgrades invalid_request / unknown to insufficient_credits, as in veo.ts. Customers see only `<provider>/<class>` (20.11). Env: `BYTEPLUS_API_KEY` (registers the adapter; BYOC provider `seedance`), optional `SEEDANCE_MODEL`, `SEEDANCE_LONG_MODEL`, `BYTEPLUS_ARK_BASE_URL` (must be `https://ark.<region>.bytepluses.com/api/v3`); both .env.example files, the setup checker (key: one unbroken token of 32+ characters with dots allowed, matching the operator's ~179-character key), the provider canary (`CANARY_BYTEPLUS_API_KEY`, workflow `platform-canary.yml`), the staging gate `--live-providers` entry `seedance` (one 4 s 720p 9:16 clip ≈ $0.30), the kill switch (`seedance` in `PROVIDER_IDS`, admin types, demo admin), runbooks (go-live 11.3 new, 11.2 reworded; provider-outage; vps-deploy table; SLO canary secrets), docs-site/go-live.html regenerated, demo queues/tour data, the sub-processor list (BytePlus Pte. Ltd. row). Tests: request building (model by tier and duration, ratio / duration / resolution, audio off, adaptive ratio on 2.5 image-to-video, base URL), submit → queued → running → succeeded → URL + billed cost, failed / expired / cancelled / 404, every error class (HTTP codes and task errors, out-of-credit and arrears text), cost per model and tier, cancel (queued / running / finished), health, env options, registry + BYOC, router order and failover both ways (Seedance → Veo → Runway → Luma; Runway/Luma problems never block Seedance; Seedance arrears / not-activated / kill switch → next provider; content refusal does not fail over), `supportsRequest` limits, env validation, kill switch.
- DECISION (operator-approved routing 2026-10-02): every tier tries seedance → veo → runway → luma (`kling` left at the end of the lists for the Kling branch to place). The plan tier chooses the Seedance model rather than the order, through `planTier` on the request (one line in `runProvider`), so router.ts only changed its two candidate lists.
- DECISION: default model `dreamina-seedance-2-0-mini-260615`. It is generally available (no preview label in the model list), the cheapest current model and BytePlus's named replacement for the retired Seedance 1.5 pro; neither the model list nor the pricing page claims a quality difference between 2.0 mini and 2.0 fast. `SEEDANCE_MODEL=dreamina-seedance-2-0-fast-260128` switches STANDARD to fast ($0.121/s).
- DECISION: list prices for every estimate. BytePlus's promotion (enterprise accounts only, until 2026-10-07 14:00 UTC+8: 2.0 mini 60 % off, 2.0 fast 25 % off, until a token cap) is noted in seedance.ts but ignored, so budgets do not break when it ends. The actual charge is `usage.completion_tokens` × the list rate (`metadata.costPence`), which overstates spend during the promotion.
- DECISION: `generate_audio: false`. The composer mutes generated clips (edl.ts). For the 2.x models the price does not depend on audio (only the retired 1.5 pro was cheaper silent), so this saves bandwidth, not money.
- DECISION: `execution_expires_after: 3600` (the documented minimum; default 48 h). Our poller gives up after 15 minutes; a task still queued an hour later expires instead of producing (and billing) a clip nobody collects. Cancel works only while queued, so a timed-out RUNNING task can still be billed (like Luma/Veo/HeyGen).
- DECISION: Seedance 1.5 pro is not offered: the model list marks `seedance-1-5-pro-251215` Retired (replacement 2.0 mini). Seedance 1.0 pro / pro fast are not offered either (older, 1080p default; no operator request).
- LEGAL REVIEW: `content/legal/en-GB/subprocessors.md` has a new row "BytePlus Pte. Ltd. (ModelArk, Seedance)": company incorporated in Singapore (registered office 1 Raffles Quay, #26-10, South Tower, Singapore 048583, per the BytePlus Terms of Service, last updated 2022-08-23; governing law per the BytePlus Customer Agreement); processing in ap-southeast-1 (Johor, Malaysia; the only region with Seedance); "BytePlus ModelArk data processing" says content that triggers the safety filter is retained for 180 days in Malaysia and Customer Data is not used for training without authorisation. The safeguard column says "SCCs": NOT CONFIRMED — neither Singapore nor Malaysia has UK adequacy, and the BytePlus DPA / transfer mechanism (IDTA or UK Addendum to the EU SCCs) must be checked by the solicitor before Seedance is enabled in production. Also review the privacy policy's international-transfer wording (Asia is new).
- UNCONFIRMED (verify with the first live run, `staging-gate --live-providers --only seedance`): (1) the HTTP error body envelope (we read `{ error: { code, message } }`, the shape the task object documents; the error-code page lists Type and Code but no JSON example); (2) the list endpoint's `page_size` range (1 used; the operator's live call used `page_size=1` and got 200); (3) typical latency (none documented; 120 s assumed for routing deadlines); (4) the exact `model` string returned by the retrieve call (we price the actual cost only when it equals a known model id, else the submit estimate stands); (5) whether the 2.x models bill the same with and without audio (the price table shows no audio split); (6) the "real faces" restriction: Seedance 2.x refuses reference images of real people (`InputImageSensitiveContentDetected.PrivacyInformation` → content_policy, which does not fail over). No pipeline path sends image_to_video today, so nothing changes now; if source frames from customer photos are added later, route them past Seedance first.
- RISK: Seedance now takes every AI clip first. If BytePlus is down or the balance runs out, clips fail over to Veo (needs `GOOGLE_GEMINI_API_KEY`), then Runway and Luma; BytePlus suspends models 2 hours after the account goes into arrears, so keep a prepaid buffer (go-live 11.3).

[2026-10-02] [20.22] Quality gate fixes from QA run 3 (project cmuqw30wy0000rp07uvgvuiud, TikTok 9:16, 30 s: QUALITY_FAILED on tiktok/black_frames 7.2–8.0 s, 25.8–26.3 s, 27.7–30.0 s and tiktok/caption_sync "Meeting panic mode" … "words not found in the narration"). **black_frames**: cause confirmed in the EDL. The MOTION_GRAPHICS shot (5–8 s) was drawn on #111111 (the default with no brand colours), the TEXT_CARD (26–30 s) on #000000, and a `fade` out with no overlap dips to the timeline background, which Shotstack defaults to #000000 (Edit API https://shotstack.io/docs/api/ Timeline.background, read 2026-10-02; fade lasts 1 s, fadeFast 0.5 s). New `pipeline/edl-backdrop.ts`: `backdropColour()` keeps the brand background colour when its Rec. 709 luma is ≥ 0.2, lifts a darker one towards white just past that (same hue; black → #333333) and uses a neutral slate #3A4150 when there is none; `readableTextColour()` keeps the brand text colour only at WCAG AA contrast, else white or near-black. Text cards (HtmlAsset `background`), motion-graphics backgrounds (ShapeAsset `fill.color`, `motionPalette`) and `timeline.background` (so fades and clips that end early show the backdrop) use it; the composition summary keeps the brand colours for brand_kit and records `brand.backdropColour`. **Check review** (`media-probe.ts`): `blackdetectFilter()` pins pix_th 0.10 and pic_th 0.98 (the filter defaults, documented with the limited-range arithmetic; the backdrop luma floor is twice pix_th) and d = BLACK_FRAME_MAX_SEC. The 0.5 s limit is spec 13.1 ("black > 500 ms") and was kept: an intentional dip of up to 0.5 s passes, real gaps still fail. The parser now accepts %g exponents. **caption_sync**: the old heuristic (subtitle-group preset + ≥ 50 % of words spoken) checked body-shot headlines (suggested on the subtitle_box preset) and ignored TikTok narration captions (hook_tiktok_native preset). DECISION: add-only column `text_overlays.kind` (`on_screen` by default, `caption` for the narration captions `overlays/voice-captions.ts` writes); migration 20261007010000_text_overlay_kind backfills `caption` from `project.metadata.voiceCaptions[shotId].overlayIds`; no new table (CI table count unchanged). caption_sync judges `kind = caption` and karaoke overlays only (`overlays/kind.ts` `mirrorsNarration`, `quality-brand-gate.ts` `spokenCaptions`). **Force-approve**: black_frames, caption_sync and loudness were already severity `error`, force-approvable with `studio:render:force-approve` (owner/admin); only a content-safety `block` is hard. The quality panel now says so: owners and admins see "These checks are advisory…" above the override, other roles "…an owner or admin can force-approve it", and a block shows "…only PostMind staff" with no override form (the server refused it anyway). 3 keys in 11 locales + review lists. Tests: edl-backdrop.test.ts (the production shot list gets a non-black full-frame fill over 5–8 s and 26–30 s with and without a black brand; timeline background; brand colours unchanged in the summary; text contrast), media-probe.test.ts (filter, parsing incl. an interval to the end of the file and exponents), quality-gate-qa3.test.ts (headlines ignored; narration captions checked: in sync, late, wrong words, no timing; karaoke; 0.3 s and 0.5 s pass, the QA 3 gaps fail and stay force-approvable), integration text-overlay-kind-migration.test.ts (backfill on PGlite), quality-panel.test.tsx (the three states), golden QA3-01 (the production shot list with an out-of-credit avatar provider, seeded presets and a no-black inspector reaches READY_FOR_REVIEW; with the old caption logic it reproduces the production caption_sync failure). Not changed: slideshow EDL backgrounds (slide colours are chosen by the user).
[2026-10-02] [20.21] Hive removed completely (operator decision 2026-10-02: "remove Hive from dependency completely"; production had only a DUMMY `HIVE_V3_SECRET_KEY`, and since 20.19 every video waited for a staff ALLOW in Admin → Safety). Studio now runs with no Hive account or key. Quality gate (`queue/workers/run-quality-gate.ts`): with no `content_safety` candidate (the router list is now empty) the scan is skipped without routing — the check is `{ code: 'content_safety', status: 'not_run', severity: 'info', detailKey: 'safetyNotScanned', detailParams: { reason: 'no_provider' } }`, the project gets `metadata.contentSafety = { state: 'skipped', reason: 'no_provider' }`, and the run continues to READY_FOR_REVIEW (then auto-approval where the review policy allows, and "Generation complete"). No Trust & Safety review, no block, no QUALITY_FAILED, no provider call and no operator alert. All other checks (duration, black frames, loudness, aspect, codec, audio/caption sync, watermark, brand kit) are unchanged; a future provider that is configured but errors non-retryably still fails closed (block). Removed: `providers/hive.ts`, `hive-v3.ts`, `hive-config.ts`, `hive-results.ts`, `hive-scan.ts` (+ tests), `pipeline/content-safety-async.ts` (+ test), `api/hive-webhook.ts` and `POST /api/studio/webhooks/hive`, the Hive registry/BYOC/canary/kill-switch (`PROVIDER_IDS`)/admin-types entries, the staging-gate `--live-providers` Hive test and its manual follow-up, `PipelineConfig.hiveCallbackBaseUrl` / `hiveAsyncTimeoutMs` / `hiveApiVersion`, the Hive V3 default of 30 month-plan starts a day (`STUDIO_CONTENT_PLAN_DAILY_STARTS` unset = unlimited), `ContentSafetyRequest.callbackUrl`, env vars `HIVE_API_KEY`, `HIVE_V3_SECRET_KEY`, `HIVE_API_VERSION`, `HIVE_V3_MAX_FRAMES`, `HIVE_ASYNC_TIMEOUT_MIN`, `STUDIO_PUBLIC_CALLBACK_BASE_URL` (both .env.example files, compose.yml, CI workflows `CANARY_HIVE_API_KEY` / `HIVE_API_KEY`). Kept (generic): the `content_safety` capability, `ContentSafetyScan` (moved to `providers/content-safety.ts`), the block/review policy in `quality-checks.ts`, the Trust & Safety review queue (script reviews still use it) and the monthly safety audit (relabelled "safety miss rate"). Setup checker (`src/lib/setup/env-file.ts`): no content-safety key is required; leftover `RETIRED_KEYS` lines are a `warn` ("is no longer used (Hive was removed); delete it"), never missing/malformed. UI: `QualityPanel` hides a not-run content-safety check from customers and shows staff a neutral "Not scanned: no content-safety scan is set up." (`review.quality.details.safetyNotScanned`, 11 locales); the Admin safety-queue / audit texts no longer name Hive (11 locales). Existing data: `releaseNoProviderSafetyReview` (`services/safety-reviews.ts`, sharing the gate-finishing code with the staff ALLOW path) closes a PENDING content review whose every flag is 20.19's "Scan could not run: no content-safety provider available" as ALLOWED by `system:studio-safety` (atomic claim, audited `studio.safety_review.release`), rewrites those checks to "Not scanned" and finishes the gate; the worker does this on its next quality-gate pass, and `scripts/ops/release-safety-holds.ts` (`--dry-run`, `--limit`, `--org`; logic in `src/lib/studio/ops/release-safety-holds.ts`) releases the rest. Docs: runbooks content-safety-miss (new "Releasing runs parked by 20.19" section), go-live §11.1 (nothing to set), provider-outage, render-deploy, review-publish-automation, slo-and-launch-readiness, staging-gate, vps-deploy; docs-site/go-live.html regenerated; CLAUDE.md layer 8; demo data and tour. Cost baseline re-recorded without the 1p Hive scan per render (test/cost/cost-baseline.json, −2 % to −8 %). Tests: quality gate with no provider (unit, `test/db/pipeline.test.ts`, golden GF-05 now expects skip-and-continue: READY_FOR_REVIEW, PASSED renders with `not_run`, no safety review, no content_safety provider job, no customer safety notification), GF-06 (a 20.19-parked run released by the worker's next gate pass, idempotent), the release script (unit: args, detection, dry run vs run; PGlite: dry run changes nothing, release → READY_FOR_REVIEW, real flags left for staff, re-run safe, another failed check → QUALITY_FAILED), setup checker (no Hive requirement, leftovers warn only), registry (old Hive keys register nothing), router/canary/live-provider lists, review policy, QualityPanel customer vs staff; A4-01 long-form now reaches review with no async scan; AA-02 BLOCK now exercised on a script review; GA-03's content-flag branch dropped (no scan can flag a render).
- DECISION: "Not scanned" does not stop automatic approval (`automation/review-policy.ts`): the operator asked for the run to continue "normally to review" under the project's existing review policy, and month plans ("Generate and schedule") depend on auto-approval to publish. A flagged or failed scan, or a render with no content-safety check at all, still needs a person; the trusted-creator threshold, ENTERPRISE, organisation policy and script-safety verdicts are unchanged. Operator: say if you want every unscanned video to wait for a person instead (one condition in `decideAutoApproval`).
- DECISION: no scan means no T&S hold at all, including a future provider that is registered but unavailable (NoProviderAvailableError / ProvidersUnavailableError → skipped). Only a provider that answers with a non-retryable error fails closed.
- DECISION: the scan path stays generic for a future provider (`content_safety` router list, `ContentSafetyScan`, block/review classes, the T&S content review). Removing it too would mean rebuilding it if a provider is ever added; nothing calls a provider today.
- DECISION: `studio.content_safety_tasks` (13.25 Hive async) and the `ContentSafetyTaskState` enum stay in the schema (no destructive migration); nothing writes them now except the business hard delete / purge, which still clean old rows. Old `system_flags` keys `studio.disabledProvider.hive` and any BYOC `provider_credentials` rows with providerId `hive` are ignored (unknown ids are skipped).
- DECISION: a released hold is closed as ALLOWED with an explicit system note, not deleted, so Admin → Safety (state ALLOWED) and the audit log show what happened. The creator gets the usual "Generation complete", not a "passed its content-safety review" message (there was no review).
- LEGAL REVIEW: Hive (Castle Global, Inc.) removed from `content/legal/en-GB/subprocessors.md` (section 2 renamed "AI generation and transcription", "Last updated" 2 October 2026). Still to review with the solicitor, since no content-safety provider is used now: acceptable-use.md 8.1 ("automated tools, including our content-safety provider"), terms.md 8.6 ("including through our content-safety provider"), dpa.md Annex security measure 8 ("automated checks … by a specialist provider"), privacy.md processing table (content safety row) and 5.1 / 5.2 (providers "to check content safety"; "automated content-safety checks can hold a video"). They were left unchanged here because the legal text is the operator's.
- NOT VERIFIED: the release script against production data (run it with `--dry-run` first); queue-backed auto-approval after a release runs only with Redis (CI).

[2026-10-02] [20.20] Google Veo 3.1 as the third text-to-video provider (operator decision 2026-10-02; OpenAI's Sora API was discontinued 2026-09-24). `src/lib/studio/providers/veo.ts` implements ProviderAdapter over the Gemini API (docs read 2026-10-02, cited in the file): `POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model}:predictLongRunning` with `x-goog-api-key`, poll `GET /v1beta/{operation name}` until `done`, video at `response.generateVideoResponse.generatedSamples[0].video.uri`; health = `models.get`; no documented cancel (NotImplementedError, like Luma). text_to_video + image_to_video (start frame sent inline as base64). Shots map to 4/6/8 s (next length up, the composer trims), 720p, 16:9 / 9:16 (4:5 → 9:16, 1:1 → 16:9, composer "cover" crops); shots over 8 s are skipped by the router via a new optional `supportsRequest()` adapter hook (`capability_unsupported`). Cost = seconds × 720p rate (Fast $0.10/s default, Standard $0.40/s, Lite $0.05/s) in pence; blocked clips are not billed. Errors map to the 20.11 classes: 401/403/PERMISSION_DENIED and invalid-key 400s → auth; 402 and FAILED_PRECONDITION (billing) → insufficient_credits; 429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED → rate_limited, or account_limit for daily-quota / spend-cap messages; safety blocks (400 text or `raiMediaFilteredCount`) → content_policy. Output URIs need the API key, so adapters may now expose `fetchOutput()`: the run result carries it and Layer 3 (`generate-asset.ts` → `copyUrlToStorage`) downloads Veo clips with the key, which is only sent to the Gemini host (redirects elsewhere get no key). Env: `GOOGLE_GEMINI_API_KEY` (registers the adapter; also a BYOC provider `veo`), optional `VEO_MODEL`, `VEO_PERSON_GENERATION`; both .env.example files, the setup checker, the provider canary (`CANARY_GOOGLE_GEMINI_API_KEY`), the staging gate live list (`veo`, adapter kind), runbooks (go-live 11.2, vps-deploy table, provider-outage, SLO canary secrets), docs-site/go-live.html, demo tour/queues data and the sub-processor list (Google LLC row). Kill switch: `veo` was already in PROVIDER_IDS / admin types / demo admin. Tests: request building and mapping, submit → running → done → URI, safety filter, every error class (HTTP + operation), cost per model, keyed download and redirect handling, env options, registry, router order + failover (Runway and Luma breakers, account failover through runProvider to Veo), 10 s skip, kill switch, env validation.
- DECISION: router order. AI_CLIP STANDARD = luma → runway → veo → kling; PLUS/ENTERPRISE = runway → luma → veo → kling (was veo → runway → luma → kling with Veo unbuilt). Veo is a failover by default, per the operator; spec 6.4 put "Veo" first on PLUS.
- DECISION: default model `veo-3.1-fast-generate-preview` (Google: suited to programmatic social content; 8 s ≈ $0.80, close to Runway gen4.5 ≈ $0.96). `VEO_MODEL` switches to Standard or Lite; an unknown model is a ConfigurationError (no price row = no budget check).
- DECISION: personGeneration. Image-to-video always sends `allow_adult` (the only documented value). Text-to-video sends `VEO_PERSON_GENERATION` (default `allow_adult`), because Google allows only `allow_adult` in the EU, UK, CH and MENA and our server is in Germany; `allow_all` is for a server outside those regions.
- 20.19 merge: Veo applies the shared out-of-credit rule (`isOutOfCreditMessage`): an `invalid_request` / `unknown` HTTP or operation error whose message says the balance is gone becomes `insufficient_credits` (failover + account hold + alert), as `classifyHttpFailure` does for adapters without their own classifier. 20.19's presenterless clip (avatar fallback) routes as AI_CLIP, so Veo is its third option too.
- DECISION: native audio cannot be turned off on the Gemini API (the official SDK rejects `generateAudio` outside Vertex). No change needed: the composer already mutes every generated clip (edl.ts VideoAsset volume 0; only USER_UPLOAD keeps its sound), so narration and music are not doubled.
- UNCONFIRMED (to verify with the first live run, `staging-gate --live-providers --only veo` or a real shot): (1) image encoding: the REST guide shows `image: { inlineData: { mimeType, data } }` but the official SDK sends `{ bytesBase64Encoded, mimeType }`; we follow the SDK. (2) `durationSeconds` as a JSON number (SDK) vs the string form in the parameter table. (3) Exact error message wording (safety, invalid key, daily quota vs per-minute) is not documented; classification matches loosely on Google status names and message text. (4) Whether the EU/UK personGeneration rule follows the caller's (server's) location; we assume it does. (5) Veo rate limits (RPM/RPD) are only shown per project in AI Studio, not documented. (6) The download redirect target host. (7) The Gemini API key format (standard vs authorization keys) is not documented, so the checker only checks it is one unbroken string. (8) "Veo 3.1 through the generateContent API" in the video overview vs `predictLongRunning` in every Veo REST sample; we use predictLongRunning. (9) The new Google LLC sub-processor row needs legal review.

[2026-10-02] [20.17] Library previews with sound (operator decision 2026-10-01, backlog 20.17: reference-library previews play with sound; the operator confirmed every corpus video is owned or licensed). `media-probe.previewClip` now keeps the first audio stream as AAC stereo 96 kb/s (`previewClipArgs`: `-map 0:v:0 -map 0:a:0? -c:a aac -b:a 96k -ac 2`, no `-an`, `+faststart` kept); everything else from A3.10 stays: 360 px wide, at most 30 s, 15 fps, 10-minute signed URL per request, the source never reaches users. Library detail player: no `muted`, controls, no autoplay, `controlsList="nodownload"`, `disablePictureInPicture`, no context menu; "Muted preview of …" label renamed in all 11 locales. Hover previews on cards (browse, recommended, similar) stay muted autoplay; Create's reference banner renders no video. One-off `scripts/library/rebuild-previews.ts` (`--dry-run`, `--limit N`, `--concurrency N` default 2 capped by `STUDIO_FFMPEG_MAX_CONCURRENT`, `--only-missing-audio`) regenerates the preview of every live item and overwrites `previewKey(s3Key)` with ingest's content type and Cache-Control; logic in `src/lib/studio/ops/rebuild-previews.ts`. Runbook: runbooks/corpus-ingestion.md "Preview sound". Tests: `previewClipArgs` (audio codec, optional audio map, no `-an`), real-ffmpeg previews with and without an audio stream (`test/media/scenes.test.ts`, CI), detail player not muted + nodownload, rebuild (dry run, limit, paging, skip when the preview already has audio or the source is silent, failure tolerance, never the source key, concurrency, content type and Cache-Control).
- DECISION (operator 2026-10-01): previews have sound. This replaces the "muted" part of Addendum A3.10 / BACKLOG 10.7 only; the low-res, 30-second, short-lived-URL, no-download rules stand.
- DECISION: the audio map is optional (`0:a:0?`), so a silent source still gives a valid video-only MP4; with no audio stream the `-c:a` options apply to nothing. Verified against real ffmpeg in CI (`test/media/scenes.test.ts`, both cases); not run locally (no ffmpeg on this machine).
- DECISION: overwrite the existing preview key instead of a new key: previews are only served through 10-minute per-request signed URLs, so the `immutable` Cache-Control never keeps an old silent copy beyond one signed URL; nothing in the database changes. No library cache version bump: the cache stores no preview data (only the source key, from which the preview key is derived).
- DECISION: `--only-missing-audio` also skips items whose source has no audio (a rebuild would stay silent), so re-runs converge; a preview that cannot be probed (missing) is rebuilt. A source key without `.mp4` (where `previewKey` would equal the source key) is refused so the source can never be overwritten.
- NOT VERIFIED: no run against production storage yet; follow the runbook (`--dry-run`, `--limit 5`, then `--only-missing-audio`).

[2026-10-02] [20.18] Vague briefs no longer dead-end generation (production bug, operator 2026-10-02: "video generation is not working"; both recent projects, "space video" and "social media automation platform.", stopped in DRAFT with `brief_too_vague` and nothing showed the suggested directions, so Generate resent the same brief). The project page (Review) now shows a "Choose a direction" panel (`src/components/studio/review/directions-panel.tsx`) for a DRAFT project whose reason is `brief_too_vague`: the up-to-three suggestions as cards with "Use this direction", and the brief in an editable box with "Generate with my changes"; both call generate with `directionChosen: true`; errors stay in the panel; viewers without `studio:project:write` see the directions and a note, no buttons. GET /projects/:id returns `directionOptions` (cleaned, only while waiting for a choice). The projects list shows the reason sentence under a vague project and links to `/projects/:id#directions`. Ideation is less strict (picks a direction itself for any named topic) and never says "too vague" twice in a row. A gentle "This is quite short…" hint (`src/lib/client/brief-hint.ts` + `src/components/studio/brief-hint.tsx`, never blocking) sits under Create's brief (video, slideshow topic and upload brief share it), each month-plan post's brief and onboarding's first video. 11 locales. Tests: brief-hint, ideation prompt, vague-brief helpers, generate input/metadata, directions panel + list + hint components, DB-backed API test (`test/api/vague-brief.test.ts`), e2e `e2e/qa/vague-brief.spec.ts`.
- DECISION: `actionable=false` is reserved for empty or meaningless requests ("make a video", "hi", one generic word) with no business facts to build on; a request that names any topic gets a direction picked by the model from the business facts, audience and tone (IDEATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT, schema description).
- DECISION: "never twice in a row" is enforced in code, not only in the prompt: `metadata.lastBriefVague` is set when ideation answers "too vague" and cleared after the next plan; that flag or the run's `metadata.directionChosen` adds DIRECTION_CHOSEN_INSTRUCTION to the prompt, and if the model still answers "too vague" `forceActionable` (`pipeline/vague-brief.ts`) builds the brief from its own fields, else the first suggested direction or the owner's text (deterministic, no second model call, no extra cost). So a plain "Generate again" after a vague answer also proceeds.
- DECISION: choosing a direction replaces the project's brief (`description`) with that direction (generate's `rawInput`), as the operator specified; `directionChosen` is per run (a later plain generate clears it) and is audited on `studio.project.generate`.
- DECISION: the ideation prompt's `Business:` line is now the business's own name (from `studio.businesses`, via the copy context); it used to carry the project name, so the model read "Business: space video". The project name has its own `Project name:` line.
- DECISION: the suggestions are Studio's own model output about the owner's brief, not provider error text, so customers see them; the failure presenter still reduces the reason to the code `brief_too_vague` for customers, and the UI matches on that code.
- DECISION: hint heuristic: fewer than 4 meaningful words (filler and the words for video/post/content/slideshow in the UI languages do not count); Chinese and Japanese runs count one word per two characters so a sentence there is never flagged; an empty field shows nothing; it is a `role="status"` note tied to the field with aria-describedby.
- DECISION: "Plan my month" has no theme or goal field on main (the month form takes dates, posts a day, the mix and accounts; Claude drafts topics from the business). The hint is on each planned post's brief (edit and "Add a post") instead.
- DECISION: when the queue is down, generate's rollback now restores the brief and metadata too (not only state and reason), so a "choose a direction" project keeps its suggestions after a failed click.
- FINDING: the Review screen showed a project's reason only for FAILED / REJECTED / QUALITY_FAILED, so a DRAFT reason was never visible on the project page; the panel now covers `brief_too_vague`.
- FIXED in the same item (operator request 2026-10-02): `restricted_topics` (spec 13.3) had the same dead end — the project rested in DRAFT with `metadata.pendingRestrictedTopics`, but no UI listed the topics or sent `confirmRestrictedTopics`. The project page now shows "Check the restricted topics" (`review/restricted-topics-panel.tsx`): the topics, "Continue anyway" (generate with `confirmRestrictedTopics: true`, audited) and the editable brief (generate with the new text, which ideation checks again); write-gated like the directions panel. GET /projects/:id returns `pendingRestrictedTopics` (cleaned: unique, ≤ 10, ≤ 120 characters; only while waiting); the projects list links such a project to `#restricted-topics`. Both panels share `review/generate-brief.tsx` (generate call, inline error, brief editor with the hint).
- DECISION: the restricted topics are shown to customers: they are the organisation's own brand-kit / business-profile list, matched by ideation, not provider text. A generate clears `pendingRestrictedTopics` (the run answers them; the queue-down rollback restores them).
- FINDING: `restrictedTopicsConfirmed` stays true on the project once given (pre-existing behaviour, unchanged), so a later edited brief that touches restricted topics is not asked about again on that project.
- VERIFIED locally: `e2e/qa/vague-brief.spec.ts` (6 tests, including the restricted-topics panel) and `e2e/qa/create.spec.ts` (9) pass against a production build on PGlite with no usable queue (the 502 rollback path); the queued path runs in CI (Redis 7).
- NOT VERIFIED: the new prompt against live Claude (no paid calls); the two production projects need one more Generate (or a direction choice) to recover.

[2026-10-02] [QA-1] Get started / Settings / Account / Export / sign-in QA (inventory + e2e/qa/onboarding-settings.*.spec.ts, 43 Playwright tests through the real UI against a production build): fixed an expired or invalid verification link landing on a bare sign-in page (middleware now sends /welcome?error=… to /verify-email), the tenant cache serving the previous organisation for 30 s after switching or creating one (cache key now includes the session's active organisation), a deleted organisation staying usable for its members for 30 s (delete drops their cached contexts), a stale business id from another organisation skipping the wizard's first-business step and the typed-id box flashing in the header while the list loads, an export left Queued forever when the queue refused the job (one-at-a-time slot never freed), the export list showing expired links as Ready with a dead Download, the seat-limit upgrade dialog talking about a monthly allowance and offering a top-up, and sign-in screens having no language switcher. CI e2e job now runs the Resend outbox mailer with placeholders so the specs can read each email from studio.email_outbox.
[2026-10-02] [20.19] Avatar presenter fallback and out-of-credit classification (production QA 2026-10-02: HeyGen `avatar_video FAILED errorClass=unknown "MOVIO_PAYMENT_INSUFFICIENT_CREDIT: Insufficient credit. This operation requires 'api' credits."` on shots 3, 7 and 10 failed the whole video with `asset_generation_failed`; a second run failed because Runway's "You do not have enough credits to run this task." 400 was `invalid_request`, so no failover to Luma). HeyGen (`providers/heygen.ts`): codes matched case-insensitively; documented payment codes (developers.heygen.com/docs/error-codes, read 2026-10-02: insufficient_credit, trial_limit_exceeded, subscription_required, plan_upgrade_required 402, quota_exceeded 429) and the undocumented legacy `MOVIO_PAYMENT_*` / `*INSUFFICIENT_CREDIT*` codes → insufficient_credits; documented auth codes (unauthorized, forbidden, insufficient_api_key_scope, phone_verification_required) → auth; an unknown code whose message says "insufficient credit" → insufficient_credits; applied to the submit error body and the poll `failure_code`/`failure_message` (VideoDetail, OpenAPI external-api.json: free-string failure_code, example rendering_failed). Generic: `classifyHttpFailure` (`providers/provider-errors.ts`, used by `httpJson`) upgrades a 4xx that would be invalid_request/unknown to insufficient_credits when the message says the balance is gone (Runway's error matrix, docs.dev.runwayml.com/errors/errors read 2026-10-02, gives every input problem `400 {error}` and documents no credit code); a Runway task FAILED with that text is insufficient_credits too. Both now take the 20.11 path: account hold, failover in the same job (generate-asset AI_CLIP / avatar / still all call `runProvider`), one operator alert. Avatar fallback (`pipeline/avatar-fallback.ts`, `generate-asset.ts`): when no avatar_video provider is available (ProvidersUnavailableError, NoProviderAvailableError for account hold / provider kill switch / open breaker / over budget / not configured, provider-level KillSwitchTriggeredError) the AI_AVATAR shot is generated as text_to_video through the router (Runway/Luma; cost tracked, budget and cap checked) with a B-roll prompt from the voiceover line and scene (no presenter, no on-screen text, brand tone keywords), clip length clamp(ceil(shot), 2, 10) while the shot keeps its duration and the narration made for the avatar; `degradedFrom: 'avatar_video'` + `degradedReason` on the asset metadata and `providerRouting.visual`; compose-video records `metadata.degradedShots`; the review screen shows "The presenter wasn't available for this video, so those moments use video clips instead." (`PresenterFallbackNote`, 11 locales). Content refusals, invalid requests, cost-cap pauses and wider kill switches still fail as before. Content safety: with no content-safety provider (none configured, or Hive's dummy key answering 403 → auth → held) the scan used to be a BLOCK (QUALITY_FAILED, not force-approvable: every video failed); it is now review-level, so the run pauses for a Trust & Safety review (13.17) — still fail-closed. Runbook provider-outage.md updated. Tests: HeyGen submit/poll with the production payload, code table, message fallback; Runway 400 and FAILED task; classifyHttpFailure / isOutOfCreditMessage; runProvider with the real Runway adapter failing over to Luma in the same job (400 and FAILED task); avatar-fallback reasons, prompt, duration, degradedShotsOf; quality check humanReview; PresenterFallbackNote; golden GF-03 (HeyGen MOVIO credit failure → Runway clip, narration kept, degraded metadata, compose → gate → READY_FOR_REVIEW), GF-04 (HeyGen content refusal → FAILED, no clip), GF-05 (Hive 403 → safety review, not a block) on PGlite.
- DECISION: HeyGen's `MOVIO_PAYMENT_INSUFFICIENT_CREDIT` is not in HeyGen's published catalogue; it is matched by pattern (`MOVIO_PAYMENT_*`, `*INSUFFICIENT_CREDIT*`) and, for unknown codes only, by the "insufficient credit" message. Documented codes always win over message text.
- DECISION: out-of-credit wording on a 4xx is classified generically in `httpJson` rather than per adapter, because Runway (and other REST providers) document no credit code. The pattern is narrow ("not enough credits", "insufficient credit/balance/funds", "out of credits", "credit balance exhausted"); 401/402/403/429 and adapter refinements are unchanged.
- DECISION: a degraded avatar shot keeps `visualTreatment: AI_AVATAR` (no schema change; VideoShot has no metadata column, so the marker lives in `providerRouting.visual` and the asset metadata). Regenerating the shot tries the presenter again.
- DECISION: avatar degradation covers every "no avatar provider" routing outcome, including over_budget; the replacement clip is budget-checked by the router like any AI_CLIP, and a CostCapPausedError still pauses the run.
- DECISION: "no content-safety provider" now opens a Trust & Safety review instead of a block. Nothing reaches review or publishing unscanned, but each video needs a staff ALLOW while Hive's key is a dummy; the operator gets the 20.11 account alert once per provider/class/UTC day plus the per-video review notification. Async-timeout and other non-provider scan failures remain blocks.

[2026-10-01] [QA-6] Business & Images + Connections QA: inventory `e2e/qa/business-images.inventory.md`, 28 Playwright tests `e2e/qa/business-images.spec.ts` (switcher, profile, scan states, brand kits, hashtags, image library incl. empty/no stock key, learned, Connections, viewer, 375 px, dark, `ar`). Fixed: (1) viewers/creators saw write controls that ended in 403 toasts (new `WriteGate`, view-only notes; Connections needs connections:manage; header Add business needs business:manage); (2) finished scans stored raw per-image/stock lines that customers read as a wall of "This step failed": the worker now stores one coded line per kind (`scan/warnings.ts`, new codes `scan_pages_skipped`, `scan_images_skipped`, `stock_not_configured`, `stock_unavailable`), identical legacy lines show once; (3) Refresh stock claimed it started with no stock provider: 501 `stock_not_configured` + plain toast; (4) OAuth callback with an unknown/used state left the browser on raw JSON: redirects to Connections with `state_expired`; (5) Reconnect offered for platforms with no app settings; (6) viewers fired a 403 request for provider keys on Connections; (7) a business could not be renamed or given a website (new Business details card, PATCH /businesses/:id); 16 new message keys x 11 locales. CI e2e env gets placeholder TikTok/YouTube/X settings.
[2026-10-01] [20.15] Library caching (operator request 2026-10-01: catalogue the reference library cost-effectively). Shared Redis read-through cache for user library reads (`src/lib/studio/library/cache.ts`, Studio's Redis DB 3, one copy for every customer because the library is platform-wide): category tree, browse pages, detail, similar, blueprint and search pages for 15 min, recommended per (organisation, business) for 1 h, query embeddings for 30 days. Every key carries a catalogue version (`studio:library:version`) that every catalogue write INCRs: ingest worker (success and final failure), re-analysis worker, admin edit and licence change, bulk review, retire, taxonomy seed. Thumbnails are signed as of the start of the UTC day (`signingDate`, valid 25 h) so the URL is stable and browsers keep the image; new thumbnails and previews are uploaded with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=604800, immutable`, and `scripts/library/set-thumbnail-cache-headers.ts` (`--dry-run`, `--limit`, `--bucket`) backfills existing thumbnails. GET library responses send `Cache-Control: private, max-age=60`. Metric `studio_library_cache_total{cache,result}`. Runbook: runbooks/corpus-ingestion.md "Library caching". Tests: cache wrapper (hit, miss, version bump, Redis failure fallback), stable signing against the real presigner, embedding cache, version bump on every write path (unit + PGlite API test), backfill script with a mocked S3 client.
- DECISION: version-in-key invalidation instead of deleting keys: a write is one INCR, nothing is scanned, old keys expire by TTL. A failed bump is logged (not throttled) and never fails the write; readers can then be up to 15 min stale.
- DECISION: cached values never hold signed URLs or attribution; detail caches main's trimmed allow-list shape (A3.10) plus the storage location for signing, which is stripped from every response.
- DECISION: licence terms are cached raw and the allowed modes are computed per response (`effectiveAllowedModes`), so a licence that expires while cached is never offered; the blueprint is withheld per response the same way.
- DECISION: thumbnail signing window 24 h (UTC day) + 1 h margin = 25 h validity, well under the R2 / SigV4 7-day cap (checked in tests). `signingDate` is `RequestPresigningArguments.signingDate` (`@smithy/types` dist-types/signature.d.ts `SigningArguments`, SDK 3.1141.0). Other `signedUrl` callers are unchanged (the option is a 4th, optional argument); previews stay 10-minute per-request URLs.
- DECISION: the query embedding is keyed by sha256(`openai:text-embedding-3-large:1536` + normalised query) (NFKC, trimmed, single spaces, lower case) and stored as float32 base64 (8 KB instead of ~30 KB JSON). Only OpenAI's vectors are cached; the search itself embeds the normalised query so a hit and a miss rank identically.
- DECISION: every search and browse cache key includes all of main's filters (#54: category exact-or-child, duration, mood, tags); tags are sorted and mood lower-cased so equivalent requests share a key.
- DECISION: staff reads (`/admin/library/*`, including the new `/admin/library/categories`) stay uncached so staff always see the database. `STUDIO_LIBRARY_CACHE=off` turns the cache off; without REDIS_URL it is off.
- DECISION: no in-process memo for the category tree: it would need the version read anyway (it is the only Redis round trip it would save) or else show a seeded taxonomy up to the memo's age late.
- FINDING: there is no unretire path in Studio today (retire is one-way), so none needed a bump; a future unretire must call `cache.bump`. After a database restore or manual SQL, bump by hand (runbook).
- FINDING: recommended stays cached up to 1 h after a website re-scan changes the business profile (accepted: the operator asked for per-business 1 h caching).
- NOT VERIFIED: no live R2 run of the backfill or of cached thumbnails in a browser; R2 supports CopyObject with x-amz-metadata-directive and Cache-Control per https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/api/s3/api/ (read 2026-10-01). Run `--dry-run` then `--limit 20` on staging first.

Fixed: approval workflow steps naming roles no standalone member can hold (`client_reviewer`, `legal`, `viewer`...; the form's default new step was `client_reviewer`) left a review waiting forever; /publications never refreshed a post in flight.
